n24 macos/Sources/Features/Terminal/BaseTerminalController.swift:282–316 DIU
   282     func newSplit(
   283         at oldView: Ghostty.SurfaceView,
   284         direction: SplitTree<Ghostty.SurfaceView>.NewDirection,
   285         baseConfig config: Ghostty.SurfaceConfiguration? = nil
   286     ) -> Ghostty.SurfaceView? {
   287         // We can only create new splits for surfaces in our tree.
   288         guard surfaceTree.root?.node(view: oldView) != nil else { return nil }
   289 
   290         // Create a new surface view
   291         guard let ghostty_app = ghostty.app else { return nil }
   292         let newView = Ghostty.SurfaceView(ghostty_app, baseConfig: config)
   293 
   294         // Do the split
   295         let newTree: SplitTree<Ghostty.SurfaceView>
   296         do {
   297             newTree = try surfaceTree.inserting(
   298                 view: newView,
   299                 at: oldView,
   300                 direction: direction)
   301         } catch {
   302             // If splitting fails for any reason (it should not), then we just log
   303             // and return. The new view we created will be deinitialized and its
   304             // no big deal.
   305             Ghostty.logger.warning("failed to insert split: \(error, privacy: .public)")
   306             return nil
   307         }
   308 
   309         replaceSurfaceTree(
   310             newTree,
   311             moveFocusTo: newView,
   312             moveFocusFrom: oldView,
   313             undoAction: "New Split")
   314 
   315         return newView
   316     }

n24 macos/Sources/Features/Terminal/BaseTerminalController.swift:666–688 DIU
   666     @objc private func ghosttyDidNewSplit(_ notification: Notification) {
   667         // The target must be within our tree
   668         guard let oldView = notification.object as? Ghostty.SurfaceView else { return }
   669         guard surfaceTree.root?.node(view: oldView) != nil else { return }
   670 
   671         // Notification must contain our base config
   672         let configAny = notification.userInfo?[Ghostty.Notification.NewSurfaceConfigKey]
   673         let config = configAny as? Ghostty.SurfaceConfiguration
   674 
   675         // Determine our desired direction
   676         guard let directionAny = notification.userInfo?["direction"] else { return }
   677         guard let direction = directionAny as? ghostty_action_split_direction_e else { return }
   678         let splitDirection: SplitTree<Ghostty.SurfaceView>.NewDirection
   679         switch direction {
   680         case GHOSTTY_SPLIT_DIRECTION_RIGHT: splitDirection = .right
   681         case GHOSTTY_SPLIT_DIRECTION_LEFT: splitDirection = .left
   682         case GHOSTTY_SPLIT_DIRECTION_DOWN: splitDirection = .down
   683         case GHOSTTY_SPLIT_DIRECTION_UP: splitDirection = .up
   684         default: return
   685         }
   686 
   687         newSplit(at: oldView, direction: splitDirection, baseConfig: config)
   688     }

n24 macos/Sources/Ghostty/Ghostty.App.swift:298–345 DIU
   298         static func readClipboard(
   299             _ userdata: UnsafeMutableRawPointer?,
   300             location: ghostty_clipboard_e,
   301             state: UnsafeMutableRawPointer,
   302             mimes: UnsafePointer<UnsafePointer<CChar>>,
   303             mimesLen: Int,
   304             list: Bool
   305         ) -> ghostty_clipboard_read_result_e {
   306             let surfaceView = self.surfaceUserdata(from: userdata)
   307             guard let surface = surfaceView.surface else {
   308                 return GHOSTTY_CLIPBOARD_READ_UNSUPPORTED
   309             }
   310 
   311             // Get our pasteboard
   312             guard let pasteboard = NSPasteboard.ghostty(location) else {
   313                 return GHOSTTY_CLIPBOARD_READ_UNSUPPORTED
   314             }
   315 
   316             // Gather the representation for each requested MIME type that
   317             // the pasteboard can serve. We only ever read the requested
   318             // representations so unrelated (potentially large) clipboard
   319             // contents are never loaded.
   320             var contents: [Ghostty.ClipboardContent] = []
   321             var seen = Set<String>()
   322             for i in 0..<mimesLen {
   323                 let mime = String(cString: mimes[i])
   324                 guard !seen.contains(mime) else { continue }
   325                 seen.insert(mime)
   326                 guard let data = pasteboard.ghosttyData(forMime: mime) else { continue }
   327                 contents.append(.init(mime: mime, data: data))
   328             }
   329 
   330             // The listing of available types, only gathered when requested.
   331             let available: [String] = list ? pasteboard.ghosttyAvailableMimes() : []
   332 
   333             // With nothing to serve and no listing requested there is
   334             // nothing to complete the read with.
   335             if contents.isEmpty && !list {
   336                 return GHOSTTY_CLIPBOARD_READ_UNAVAILABLE
   337             }
   338 
   339             completeClipboardRequest(
   340                 surface,
   341                 contents: contents,
   342                 available: available,
   343                 state: state)
   344             return GHOSTTY_CLIPBOARD_READ_STARTED
   345         }

n24 macos/Sources/Ghostty/Ghostty.App.swift:415–469 DIU
   415         private static func completeClipboardRequest(
   416             _ surface: ghostty_surface_t,
   417             contents: [Ghostty.ClipboardContent],
   418             available: [String],
   419             state: UnsafeMutableRawPointer,
   420             confirmed: Bool = false,
   421             remember: Bool = false
   422         ) {
   423             // Copy everything into C memory for the duration of the call.
   424             var cStrings: [UnsafeMutablePointer<CChar>] = []
   425             var cDatas: [UnsafeMutableRawPointer] = []
   426             defer {
   427                 cStrings.forEach { free($0) }
   428                 cDatas.forEach { $0.deallocate() }
   429             }
   430 
   431             var cContents: [ghostty_clipboard_content_s] = []
   432             for entry in contents {
   433                 guard let mime = strdup(entry.mime) else { continue }
   434                 cStrings.append(mime)
   435                 let buf = UnsafeMutableRawPointer.allocate(
   436                     byteCount: max(entry.data.count, 1),
   437                     alignment: 1)
   438                 cDatas.append(buf)
   439                 entry.data.withUnsafeBytes { src in
   440                     if let base = src.baseAddress {
   441                         buf.copyMemory(from: base, byteCount: src.count)
   442                     }
   443                 }
   444                 cContents.append(ghostty_clipboard_content_s(
   445                     mime: mime,
   446                     data: buf.assumingMemoryBound(to: CChar.self),
   447                     len: entry.data.count))
   448             }
   449 
   450             var cAvailable: [UnsafePointer<CChar>] = []
   451             for mime in available {
   452                 guard let str = strdup(mime) else { continue }
   453                 cStrings.append(str)
   454                 cAvailable.append(UnsafePointer(str))
   455             }
   456 
   457             cContents.withUnsafeBufferPointer { contentsBuf in
   458                 cAvailable.withUnsafeBufferPointer { availableBuf in
   459                     var complete = ghostty_clipboard_complete_s(
   460                         contents: contentsBuf.baseAddress,
   461                         contents_len: contentsBuf.count,
   462                         available: availableBuf.baseAddress,
   463                         available_len: availableBuf.count,
   464                         confirmed: confirmed,
   465                         remember: remember)
   466                     ghostty_surface_complete_clipboard_request(surface, &complete, state)
   467                 }
   468             }
   469         }

   572         static func action(_ app: ghostty_app_t, target: ghostty_target_s, action: ghostty_action_s) -> Bool {
   573             // Make sure it a target we understand so all our action handlers can assert
   574             switch target.tag {

   576                 break
   577 
   578             default:

   580                 return false
   581             }
   582 
   583             // Action dispatch
   584             switch action.tag {
   585             case GHOSTTY_ACTION_QUIT:
   586                 quit(app)
   587 
   588             case GHOSTTY_ACTION_NEW_WINDOW:
   589                 newWindow(app, target: target)
   590 
   591             case GHOSTTY_ACTION_NEW_TAB:
   592                 newTab(app, target: target)
   593 
   594             case GHOSTTY_ACTION_NEW_SPLIT:
   595                 newSplit(app, target: target, direction: action.action.new_split)

   949         private static func newTab(_ app: ghostty_app_t, target: ghostty_target_s) {

   968                     _ = alert.runModal()
   969                     return
   970                 }

   973                     name: Notification.ghosttyNewTab,

   975                     userInfo: [
   976                         Notification.NewSurfaceConfigKey: SurfaceConfiguration(from: ghostty_surface_inherited_config(surface, GHOSTTY_SURFACE_CONTEXT_TAB)),
   977                     ]
   978                 )
   979

   981                 assertionFailure()
   982             }

  1005                     ]

  1009                 assertionFailure()
  1010             }
  1011         }

n24 src/App.zig:444–529 DIU
   444 pub fn performAction(
   445     self: *App,
   446     rt_app: *apprt.App,
   447     action: input.Binding.Action.Scoped(.app),
   448 ) !void {
   449     switch (action) {
   450         .unbind => unreachable,
   451         .ignore => {},
   452         .quit => _ = try rt_app.performAction(.app, .quit, {}),
   453         .new_window => _ = try self.newWindow(rt_app, .{ .parent = null }),
   454         .open_config => |v| _ = try rt_app.performAction(
   455             .app,
   456             .open_config,
   457             switch (v) {
   458                 .os_open => .os_open,
   459                 .new_window => .new_window,
   460             },
   461         ),
   462         .reload_config => _ = try rt_app.performAction(.app, .reload_config, .{}),
   463         .set_window_theme => |v| _ = try rt_app.performAction(
   464             .app,
   465             .set_window_theme,
   466             switch (v) {
   467                 .config => .config,
   468                 .system => .system,
   469                 .light => .light,
   470                 .dark => .dark,
   471             },
   472         ),
   473         .close_all_windows => _ = try rt_app.performAction(.app, .close_all_windows, {}),
   474         .toggle_quick_terminal => _ = try rt_app.performAction(.app, .toggle_quick_terminal, {}),
   475         .toggle_visibility => _ = try rt_app.performAction(.app, .toggle_visibility, {}),
   476         .check_for_updates => _ = try rt_app.performAction(.app, .check_for_updates, {}),
   477         .show_gtk_inspector => _ = try rt_app.performAction(.app, .show_gtk_inspector, {}),
   478         .undo => _ = try rt_app.performAction(.app, .undo, {}),
   479 
   480         .redo => _ = try rt_app.performAction(.app, .redo, {}),
   481     }
   482 }
   483 
   484 /// Performs a chained action. We will continue executing each action
   485 /// even if there is a failure in a prior action.
   486 pub fn performAllChainedAction(
   487     self: *App,
   488     rt_app: *apprt.App,
   489     actions: []const input.Binding.Action,
   490 ) void {
   491     for (actions) |action| {
   492         self.performAllAction(rt_app, action) catch |err| {
   493             log.warn("error performing chained action action={s} err={}", .{
   494                 @tagName(action),
   495                 err,
   496             });
   497         };
   498     }
   499 }
   500 
   501 /// Perform an app-wide binding action. If the action is surface-specific
   502 /// then it will be performed on all surfaces. To perform only app-scoped
   503 /// actions, use performAction.
   504 pub fn performAllAction(
   505     self: *App,
   506     rt_app: *apprt.App,
   507     action: input.Binding.Action,
   508 ) !void {
   509     switch (action.scope()) {
   510         // App-scoped actions are handled by the app so that they aren't
   511         // repeated for each surface (since each surface forwards
   512         // app-scoped actions back up).
   513         .app => try self.performAction(
   514             rt_app,
   515             action.scoped(.app).?, // asserted through the scope match
   516         ),
   517 
   518         // Surface-scoped actions are performed on all surfaces. Errors
   519         // are logged but processing continues.
   520         .surface => for (self.surfaces.items) |surface| {
   521             _ = surface.core().performBindingAction(action) catch |err| {
   522                 log.warn("error performing binding action on surface id={x} err={}", .{
   523                     surface.core().id,
   524                     err,
   525                 });
   526             };
   527         },
   528     }
   529 }

n24 src/Surface.zig:895–920 DIU
   895 /// Queue a message for the IO thread, taking ownership of `msg`.
   896 ///
   897 /// We centralize all our logic into this spot so we can intercept
   898 /// messages for example in readonly mode.
   899 fn queueIo(
   900     self: *Surface,
   901     msg: termio.Message,
   902     mutex: termio.Termio.MutexState,
   903 ) void {
   904     // In readonly mode, we don't allow any writes through to the pty.
   905     if (self.readonly) {
   906         switch (msg) {
   907             .write_small,
   908             .write_stable,
   909             .write_alloc,
   910             => {
   911                 msg.deinit();
   912                 return;
   913             },
   914 
   915             else => {},
   916         }
   917     }
   918 
   919     self.io.queueMessage(msg, mutex);
   920 }

n24 src/Surface.zig:2325–2443 DIU
  2325 fn copySelectionToClipboards(
  2326     self: *Surface,
  2327     sel: terminal.Selection,
  2328     clipboards: []const apprt.Clipboard,
  2329     format: input.Binding.Action.CopyToClipboard,
  2330 ) !void {
  2331     // Create an arena to simplify memory management here.
  2332     var arena = ArenaAllocator.init(self.alloc);
  2333     defer arena.deinit();
  2334     const alloc = arena.allocator();
  2335 
  2336     // The options we'll use for all formatting. We'll just override the
  2337     // emit format.
  2338     const opts: terminal.formatter.Options = .{
  2339         .emit = .plain, // We'll override this below
  2340         .unwrap = true,
  2341         .trim = self.config.clipboard_trim_trailing_spaces,
  2342         .codepoint_map = self.config.clipboard_codepoint_map.map.list,
  2343         .background = self.io.terminal.colors.background.get(),
  2344         .foreground = self.io.terminal.colors.foreground.get(),
  2345         .palette = &self.io.terminal.colors.palette.current,
  2346     };
  2347 
  2348     const ScreenFormatter = terminal.formatter.ScreenFormatter;
  2349     var aw: std.Io.Writer.Allocating = .init(alloc);
  2350     var contents_buf: [2]apprt.ClipboardContent = undefined;
  2351     var contents: std.ArrayList(apprt.ClipboardContent) = .initBuffer(&contents_buf);
  2352     switch (format) {
  2353         .plain => {
  2354             var formatter: ScreenFormatter = .init(self.io.terminal.screens.active, opts);
  2355             formatter.content = .{ .selection = sel };
  2356             try formatter.format(&aw.writer);
  2357             contents.appendAssumeCapacity(.{
  2358                 .mime = "text/plain",
  2359                 .data = try aw.toOwnedSliceSentinel(0),
  2360             });
  2361         },
  2362 
  2363         .vt => {
  2364             var formatter: ScreenFormatter = .init(self.io.terminal.screens.active, opts: {
  2365                 var copy = opts;
  2366                 copy.emit = .vt;
  2367                 break :opts copy;
  2368             });
  2369             formatter.content = .{ .selection = sel };
  2370             try formatter.format(&aw.writer);
  2371 
  2372             // Note: We don't apply codepoint mappings to VT format since it contains
  2373             // escape sequences that should be preserved as-is
  2374             contents.appendAssumeCapacity(.{
  2375                 .mime = "text/plain",
  2376                 .data = try aw.toOwnedSliceSentinel(0),
  2377             });
  2378         },
  2379 
  2380         .html => {
  2381             var formatter: ScreenFormatter = .init(self.io.terminal.screens.active, opts: {
  2382                 var copy = opts;
  2383                 copy.emit = .html;
  2384                 break :opts copy;
  2385             });
  2386             formatter.content = .{ .selection = sel };
  2387             try formatter.format(&aw.writer);
  2388 
  2389             // Note: We don't apply codepoint mappings to HTML format since HTML
  2390             // has its own character encoding and entity system
  2391             contents.appendAssumeCapacity(.{
  2392                 .mime = "text/html",
  2393                 .data = try aw.toOwnedSliceSentinel(0),
  2394             });
  2395         },
  2396 
  2397         .mixed => {
  2398             // First, generate plain text with codepoint mappings applied
  2399             var formatter: ScreenFormatter = .init(self.io.terminal.screens.active, opts);
  2400             formatter.content = .{ .selection = sel };
  2401             try formatter.format(&aw.writer);
  2402             contents.appendAssumeCapacity(.{
  2403                 .mime = "text/plain",
  2404                 .data = try aw.toOwnedSliceSentinel(0),
  2405             });
  2406 
  2407             assert(aw.written().len == 0);
  2408             // Second, generate HTML without codepoint mappings
  2409             formatter = .init(self.io.terminal.screens.active, opts: {
  2410                 var copy = opts;
  2411                 copy.emit = .html;
  2412 
  2413                 // We purposely don't emit background/foreground for mixed
  2414                 // mode because the HTML contents is often used for rich text
  2415                 // input and with trimmed spaces it looks pretty bad.
  2416                 copy.background = null;
  2417                 copy.foreground = null;
  2418 
  2419                 break :opts copy;
  2420             });
  2421             formatter.content = .{ .selection = sel };
  2422             try formatter.format(&aw.writer);
  2423 
  2424             // Note: We don't apply codepoint mappings to HTML format
  2425             contents.appendAssumeCapacity(.{
  2426                 .mime = "text/html",
  2427                 .data = try aw.toOwnedSliceSentinel(0),
  2428             });
  2429         },
  2430     }
  2431 
  2432     assert(contents.items.len > 0);
  2433     for (clipboards) |clipboard| self.rt_surface.setClipboard(
  2434         clipboard,
  2435         contents.items,
  2436         false,
  2437     ) catch |err| {
  2438         log.err(
  2439             "error setting clipboard string clipboard={} err={}",
  2440             .{ clipboard, err },
  2441         );
  2442     };
  2443 }

n24 src/Surface.zig:2451–2473 DIU
  2451 fn setSelection(self: *Surface, sel_: ?terminal.Selection) !void {
  2452     // Compute the transition before `select` below, which untracks (frees)
  2453     // the previous selection's tracked pins; reading them after would be a
  2454     // use-after-free.
  2455     const prev_ = self.io.terminal.screens.active.selection;
  2456     const changed = changed: {
  2457         const prev = prev_ orelse break :changed sel_ != null;
  2458         const sel = sel_ orelse break :changed true;
  2459         break :changed !sel.eql(prev);
  2460     };
  2461 
  2462     try self.io.terminal.screens.active.select(sel_);
  2463 
  2464     if (changed) {
  2465         _ = self.rt_app.performAction(
  2466             .{ .surface = self },
  2467             .selection_changed,
  2468             {},
  2469         ) catch |err| {
  2470             log.warn("apprt failed selection_changed notification err={}", .{err});
  2471         };
  2472     }
  2473 }

n24 src/Surface.zig:2479–2506 DIU
  2479 fn setSelectionAndCopy(self: *Surface, sel: terminal.Selection) !void {
  2480     try self.setSelection(sel);
  2481 
  2482     switch (self.config.copy_on_select) {
  2483         .none => {},
  2484 
  2485         // The selection clipboard is set if supported, otherwise nothing is copied.
  2486         .primary => try self.copySelectionToClipboards(
  2487             sel,
  2488             &.{.selection},
  2489             .mixed,
  2490         ),
  2491 
  2492         // Only the standard clipboard is set.
  2493         .clipboard => try self.copySelectionToClipboards(
  2494             sel,
  2495             &.{.standard},
  2496             .mixed,
  2497         ),
  2498 
  2499         // Both standard and selection clipboards are set.
  2500         .both => try self.copySelectionToClipboards(
  2501             sel,
  2502             &.{ .standard, .selection },
  2503             .mixed,
  2504         ),
  2505     }
  2506 }

  2790 /// Called for any key events. This handles keybindings, encoding and
  2791 /// sending to the terminal, etc.
  2792 pub fn keyCallback(
  2793     self: *Surface,
  2794     event_orig: input.KeyEvent,
  2795 ) !InputEffect {
  2796     // log.warn("text keyCallback event={}", .{event_orig});
  2797 
  2798     // Apply key remappings to transform modifiers before any processing.
  2799     // This allows users to remap modifier keys at the app level.
  2800     var event = event_orig;
  2801     if (self.config.key_remaps.isRemapped(event_orig.mods)) {
  2802         event.mods = self.config.key_remaps.apply(event_orig.mods);
  2803     }
  2804 
  2805     // Crash metadata in case we crash in here
  2806     crash.sentry.thread_state = self.crashThreadState();
  2807     defer crash.sentry.thread_state = null;
  2808 
  2809     // Setup our inspector event if we have an inspector.
  2810     var insp_ev: ?inspectorpkg.KeyEvent = if (self.inspector != null) ev: {
  2811         var copy = event;
  2812         copy.utf8 = "";
  2813         if (event.utf8.len > 0) copy.utf8 = try self.alloc.dupe(u8, event.utf8);
  2814         break :ev .{ .event = copy };
  2815     } else null;
  2816 
  2817     // When we're done processing, we always want to add the event to
  2818     // the inspector.
  2819     defer if (insp_ev) |ev| ev: {
  2820         // We have to check for the inspector again because our keybinding
  2821         // might close it.
  2822         const insp = self.inspector orelse {
  2823             ev.deinit(self.alloc);
  2824             break :ev;
  2825         };
  2826 
  2827         if (insp.recordKeyEvent(self.alloc, ev)) {
  2828             self.queueRender() catch {};
  2829         } else |err| {
  2830             log.warn("error adding key event to inspector err={}", .{err});
  2831         }
  2832     };
  2833 
  2834     // Handle keybindings first. We need to handle this on all events
  2835     // (press, repeat, release) because a press may perform a binding but
  2836     // a release should not encode if we consumed the press.
  2837     if (try self.maybeHandleBinding(
  2838         event,
  2839         if (insp_ev) |*ev| ev else null,
  2840     )) |v| return v;
  2841     // If we allow KAM and KAM is enabled then we do nothing.
  2842     if (self.config.vt_kam_allowed) {
  2843         self.renderer_state.mutex.lockUncancelable(global.io());
  2844         defer self.renderer_state.mutex.unlock(global.io());
  2845         if (self.io.terminal.modes.get(.disable_keyboard)) return .consumed;
  2846     }
  2847 
  2848     // If this input event has text, then we hide the mouse if configured.
  2849     // We only do this on pressed events to avoid hiding the mouse when we
  2850     // change focus due to a keybinding (i.e. switching tabs).
  2851     if (self.config.mouse_hide_while_typing and
  2852         event.action == .press and
  2853         !self.mouse.hidden and
  2854         event.utf8.len > 0)
  2855     {
  2856         self.hideMouse();
  2857     }
  2858 
  2859     // If our mouse modifiers change we may need to change our
  2860     // link highlight state.
  2861     if (!self.mouse.mods.equal(event.mods)) mouse_mods: {
  2862         // Update our modifiers, this will update mouse mods too
  2863         self.modsChanged(event.mods);
  2864 
  2865         // We only refresh links if
  2866         // 1. mouse reporting is off
  2867         // OR
  2868         // 2. mouse reporting is on and we are not reporting shift to the terminal
  2869         if (self.io.terminal.flags.mouse_event == .none or
  2870             (self.mouse.mods.shift and !self.mouseShiftCapture(false)))
  2871         {
  2872             // Refresh our link state
  2873             const pos = self.rt_surface.getCursorPos() catch break :mouse_mods;
  2874             self.renderer_state.mutex.lockUncancelable(global.io());
  2875             defer self.renderer_state.mutex.unlock(global.io());
  2876             self.mouseRefreshLinks(
  2877                 pos,
  2878                 self.posToViewport(pos.x, pos.y),
  2879                 self.mouse.over_link,
  2880             ) catch |err| {
  2881                 log.warn("failed to refresh links err={}", .{err});
  2882                 break :mouse_mods;
  2883             };
  2884         } else if (self.io.terminal.flags.mouse_event != .none and !self.mouse.mods.shift) {
  2885             // If we have mouse reports on and we don't have shift pressed, we reset state
  2886             _ = try self.rt_app.performAction(
  2887                 .{ .surface = self },
  2888                 .mouse_shape,
  2889                 self.io.terminal.mouse_shape,
  2890             );
  2891             _ = try self.rt_app.performAction(
  2892                 .{ .surface = self },
  2893                 .mouse_over_link,
  2894                 .{ .url = "" },
  2895             );
  2896             try self.queueRender();
  2897         }
  2898     }
  2899 
  2900     // Process the cursor state logic. This will update the cursor shape if
  2901     // needed, depending on the key state.
  2902     if ((SurfaceMouse{
  2903         .physical_key = event.key,
  2904         .mouse_event = self.io.terminal.flags.mouse_event,
  2905         .mouse_shape = self.io.terminal.mouse_shape,
  2906         .mods = self.mouse.mods,
  2907         .over_link = self.mouse.over_link,
  2908         .hidden = self.mouse.hidden,
  2909     }).keyToMouseShape()) |shape| _ = try self.rt_app.performAction(
  2910         .{ .surface = self },
  2911         .mouse_shape,
  2912         shape,
  2913     );
  2914 
  2915     // We've processed a key event that produced some data so we want to
  2916     // track the last pressed key.
  2917     self.pressed_key = event: {
  2918         // We need to unset the allocated fields that will become invalid
  2919         var copy = event;
  2920         copy.utf8 = "";
  2921 
  2922         // If we have a previous pressed key and we're releasing it
  2923         // then we set it to invalid to prevent repeating the release event.
  2924         if (event.action == .release) {
  2925             // if we didn't have a previous event and this is a release
  2926             // event then we just want to set it to null.
  2927             const prev = self.pressed_key orelse break :event null;
  2928             if (prev.key == copy.key) copy.key = .unidentified;
  2929         }
  2930 
  2931         // If our key is invalid and we have no mods, then we're done!
  2932         // This helps catch the state that we naturally released all keys.
  2933         if (copy.key == .unidentified and copy.mods.empty()) break :event null;
  2934 
  2935         break :event copy;
  2936     };
  2937 
  2938     // Encode and send our key. If we didn't encode anything, then we
  2939     // return the effect as ignored.
  2940     if (try self.encodeKey(
  2941         event,
  2942         if (insp_ev) |*ev| ev else null,
  2943     )) |write_req| {
  2944         // If our process is exited and we press a key that results in
  2945         // an encoded value, we close the surface. We want to eventually
  2946         // move this behavior to the apprt probably.
  2947         if (self.child_exited) {
  2948             write_req.deinit();
  2949             self.close();
  2950             return .closed;
  2951         }
  2952 
  2953         self.queueIo(switch (write_req) {
  2954             .small => |v| .{ .write_small = v },
  2955             .stable => |v| .{ .write_stable = v },
  2956             .alloc => |v| .{ .write_alloc = v },
  2957         }, .unlocked);
  2958     } else {
  2959         // No valid request means that we didn't encode anything.
  2960         return .ignored;
  2961     }
  2962 
  2963     // If our event is any keypress that isn't a modifier and we generated
  2964     // some data to send to the pty, then we move the viewport down to the
  2965     // bottom. We also clear the selection for any key other then modifiers.
  2966     if (!event.key.modifier()) {
  2967         self.renderer_state.mutex.lockUncancelable(global.io());
  2968         defer self.renderer_state.mutex.unlock(global.io());
  2969 
  2970         if (self.config.selection_clear_on_typing or
  2971             event.key == .escape)
  2972         {
  2973             try self.setSelection(null);
  2974         }
  2975 
  2976         if (self.config.scroll_to_bottom.keystroke) self.io.terminal.scrollViewport(.bottom);
  2977 
  2978         try self.queueRender();
  2979     }
  2980 
  2981     return .consumed;
  2982 }

  2984 /// Maybe handles a binding for a given event and if so returns the effect.
  2985 /// Returns null if the event is not handled in any way and processing should
  2986 /// continue.
  2987 fn maybeHandleBinding(
  2988     self: *Surface,
  2989     event: input.KeyEvent,
  2990     insp_ev: ?*inspectorpkg.KeyEvent,
  2991 ) !?InputEffect {
  2992     switch (event.action) {
  2993         // Release events never trigger a binding but we need to check if
  2994         // we consumed the press event so we don't encode the release.
  2995         .release => {
  2996             if (self.keyboard.last_trigger) |last| {
  2997                 if (last == event.bindingHash()) {
  2998                     // We don't reset the last trigger on release because
  2999                     // an apprt may send multiple release events for a single
  3000                     // press event.
  3001                     return .consumed;
  3002                 }
  3003             }
  3004 
  3005             return null;
  3006         },
  3007 
  3008         // Carry on processing.
  3009         .press, .repeat => {},
  3010     }
  3011 
  3012     // Find an entry in the keybind set that matches our event.
  3013     const entry: input.Binding.Set.Entry = entry: {
  3014         // Handle key sequences first.
  3015         if (self.keyboard.sequence_set) |set| {
  3016             // Get our entry from the set for the given event.
  3017             if (set.getEvent(event)) |v| break :entry v;
  3018 
  3019             // No entry found. We need to encode everything up to this
  3020             // point and send to the pty since we're in a sequence.
  3021 
  3022             // We ignore modifiers so that nested sequences such as
  3023             // ctrl+a>ctrl+b>c work.
  3024             if (event.key.modifier()) return null;
  3025 
  3026             // If we have a catch-all of ignore, then we special case our
  3027             // invalid sequence handling to ignore it.
  3028             if (self.catchAllIsIgnore()) {
  3029                 self.endKeySequence(.drop, .retain);
  3030                 return .ignored;
  3031             }
  3032 
  3033             // Encode everything up to this point
  3034             self.endKeySequence(.flush, .retain);
  3035 
  3036             return null;
  3037         }
  3038 
  3039         // No currently active sequence, move on to tables. For tables,
  3040         // we search inner-most table to outer-most. The table stack does
  3041         // NOT include the root set.
  3042         const table_items = self.keyboard.table_stack.items;
  3043         if (table_items.len > 0) {
  3044             for (0..table_items.len) |i| {
  3045                 const rev_i: usize = table_items.len - 1 - i;
  3046                 const table = table_items[rev_i];
  3047                 if (table.set.getEvent(event)) |v| {
  3048                     // If this is a one-shot activation AND its the currently
  3049                     // active table, then we deactivate it after this.
  3050                     // Note: we may want to change the semantics here to
  3051                     // remove this table no matter where it is in the stack,
  3052                     // maybe.
  3053                     if (table.once and i == 0) _ = try self.performBindingAction(
  3054                         .deactivate_key_table,
  3055                     );
  3056 
  3057                     break :entry v;
  3058                 }
  3059             }
  3060         }
  3061 
  3062         // No table, use our default set
  3063         break :entry self.config.keybind.set.getEvent(event) orelse
  3064             return null;
  3065     };
  3066 
  3067     // Determine if this entry has an action or if its a leader key.
  3068     const leaf: input.Binding.Set.GenericLeaf = switch (entry.value_ptr.*) {
  3069         .leader => |set| {
  3070             // Store this event so that we can drain and encode on invalid.
  3071             // We don't need to cap this because it is naturally capped by
  3072             // the config validation.
  3073             if (try self.encodeKey(event, insp_ev)) |req| {
  3074                 self.keyboard.sequence_queued.append(self.alloc, req) catch |err| {
  3075                     req.deinit();
  3076                     return err;
  3077                 };
  3078             }
  3079 
  3080             // Setup the next set we'll look at only after all fallible work.
  3081             self.keyboard.sequence_set = set;
  3082 
  3083             // Start or continue our key sequence
  3084             _ = self.rt_app.performAction(
  3085                 .{ .surface = self },
  3086                 .key_sequence,
  3087                 .{ .trigger = entry.key_ptr.* },
  3088             ) catch |err| {
  3089                 log.warn(
  3090                     "failed to notify app of key sequence err={}",
  3091                     .{err},
  3092                 );
  3093             };
  3094 
  3095             return .consumed;
  3096         },
  3097 
  3098         inline .leaf, .leaf_chained => |leaf| leaf.generic(),
  3099     };
  3100 
  3101     // consumed determines if the input is consumed or if we continue
  3102     // encoding the key (if we have a key to encode).
  3103     const consumed = consumed: {
  3104         // If the consumed flag is explicitly set, then we are consumed.
  3105         if (leaf.flags.consumed) break :consumed true;
  3106 
  3107         // If the global or all flag is set, we always consume.
  3108         if (leaf.flags.global or leaf.flags.all) break :consumed true;
  3109 
  3110         break :consumed false;
  3111     };
  3112 
  3113     // We have an action, so at this point we're handling SOMETHING so
  3114     // we reset the last trigger to null. We only set this if we actually
  3115     // perform an action (below)
  3116     self.keyboard.last_trigger = null;
  3117 
  3118     // An action also always resets the sequence set.
  3119     self.keyboard.sequence_set = null;
  3120 
  3121     // Setup our actions
  3122     const actions = leaf.actionsSlice();
  3123 
  3124     // Attempt to perform the action
  3125     log.debug("key event binding flags={} action={any}", .{
  3126         leaf.flags,
  3127         actions,
  3128     });
  3129     const performed = performed: {
  3130         // If this is a global or all action, then we perform it on
  3131         // the app and it applies to every surface.
  3132         if (leaf.flags.global or leaf.flags.all) {
  3133             self.app.performAllChainedAction(
  3134                 self.rt_app,
  3135                 actions,
  3136             );
  3137 
  3138             // "All" actions are always performed since they are global.
  3139             break :performed true;
  3140         }
  3141 
  3142         // Perform each action. We are performed if ANY of the chained
  3143         // actions perform.
  3144         var performed: bool = false;
  3145         for (actions) |action| {
  3146             if (self.performBindingAction(action)) |v| {
  3147                 performed = performed or v;
  3148             } else |err| {
  3149                 log.info(
  3150                     "key binding action failed action={t} err={}",
  3151                     .{ action, err },
  3152                 );
  3153             }
  3154         }
  3155 
  3156         break :performed performed;
  3157     };
  3158 
  3159     if (performed) {
  3160         // If we performed an action and it was a closing action,
  3161         // our "self" pointer is not safe to use anymore so we need to
  3162         // just exit immediately.
  3163         for (actions) |action| if (closingAction(action)) {
  3164             log.debug("key binding is a closing binding, halting key event processing", .{});
  3165             return .closed;
  3166         };
  3167 
  3168         // If our action was "ignore" then we return the special input
  3169         // effect of "ignored".
  3170         for (actions) |action| if (action == .ignore) {
  3171             // If we're in a sequence, clear it.
  3172             self.endKeySequence(.drop, .retain);
  3173 
  3174             return .ignored;
  3175         };
  3176     }
  3177 
  3178     // If we have the performable flag and the action was not performed,
  3179     // then we act as though a binding didn't exist.
  3180     if (leaf.flags.performable and !performed) {
  3181         // If we're in a sequence, we treat this as if we pressed a key
  3182         // that doesn't exist in the sequence. Reset our sequence and flush
  3183         // any queued events.
  3184         self.endKeySequence(.flush, .retain);
  3185 
  3186         return null;
  3187     }
  3188 
  3189     // If we consume this event, then we are done. If we don't consume
  3190     // it, we processed the action but we still want to process our
  3191     // encodings, too.
  3192     if (consumed) {
  3193         // If we had queued events, we deinit them since we consumed
  3194         self.endKeySequence(.drop, .retain);
  3195 
  3196         // Store our last trigger so we don't encode the release event
  3197         self.keyboard.last_trigger = event.bindingHash();
  3198 
  3199         if (insp_ev) |ev| {
  3200             ev.binding = self.alloc.dupe(
  3201                 input.Binding.Action,
  3202                 actions,
  3203             ) catch |err| binding: {
  3204                 log.warn(
  3205                     "error allocating binding action for inspector err={}",
  3206                     .{err},
  3207                 );
  3208                 break :binding &.{};
  3209             };
  3210         }
  3211         return .consumed;
  3212     }
  3213 
  3214     // If we didn't perform OR we didn't consume, then we want to
  3215     // encode any queued events for a sequence.
  3216     self.endKeySequence(.flush, .retain);
  3217 
  3218     return null;
  3219 }

n24 src/Surface.zig:3245–3419 DIU
  3245 /// This checks if the current keybinding sets have a catch_all binding
  3246 /// with `ignore`. This is used to determine some special input cases.
  3247 fn catchAllIsIgnore(self: *Surface) bool {
  3248     // Get our catch all
  3249     const entry: input.Binding.Set.Entry = entry: {
  3250         const trigger: input.Binding.Trigger = .{ .key = .catch_all };
  3251 
  3252         const table_items = self.keyboard.table_stack.items;
  3253         for (0..table_items.len) |i| {
  3254             const rev_i: usize = table_items.len - 1 - i;
  3255             const entry = table_items[rev_i].set.get(trigger) orelse continue;
  3256             break :entry entry;
  3257         }
  3258 
  3259         break :entry self.config.keybind.set.get(trigger) orelse
  3260             return false;
  3261     };
  3262 
  3263     // We have a catch-all entry, see if its an ignore
  3264     return switch (entry.value_ptr.*) {
  3265         .leader => false,
  3266         .leaf => |leaf| leaf.action == .ignore,
  3267         .leaf_chained => |leaf| chained: for (leaf.actions.items) |action| {
  3268             if (action == .ignore) break :chained true;
  3269         } else false,
  3270     };
  3271 }
  3272 
  3273 const KeySequenceQueued = enum { flush, drop };
  3274 const KeySequenceMemory = enum { retain, free };
  3275 
  3276 /// End a key sequence. Safe to call if no key sequence is active.
  3277 ///
  3278 /// Action and mem determine the behavior of the queued inputs up to this
  3279 /// point.
  3280 fn endKeySequence(
  3281     self: *Surface,
  3282     action: KeySequenceQueued,
  3283     mem: KeySequenceMemory,
  3284 ) void {
  3285     // Notify apprt key sequence ended
  3286     _ = self.rt_app.performAction(
  3287         .{ .surface = self },
  3288         .key_sequence,
  3289         .end,
  3290     ) catch |err| {
  3291         log.warn(
  3292             "failed to notify app of key sequence end err={}",
  3293             .{err},
  3294         );
  3295     };
  3296 
  3297     // No matter what we clear our current sequence set. This restores
  3298     // the set we look at to the root set.
  3299     self.keyboard.sequence_set = null;
  3300 
  3301     // If we have no queued data, there is nothing else to do.
  3302     if (self.keyboard.sequence_queued.items.len == 0) return;
  3303 
  3304     // Run the proper action first
  3305     switch (action) {
  3306         .flush => for (self.keyboard.sequence_queued.items) |write_req| {
  3307             self.queueIo(switch (write_req) {
  3308                 .small => |v| .{ .write_small = v },
  3309                 .stable => |v| .{ .write_stable = v },
  3310                 .alloc => |v| .{ .write_alloc = v },
  3311             }, .unlocked);
  3312         },
  3313 
  3314         .drop => for (self.keyboard.sequence_queued.items) |req| req.deinit(),
  3315     }
  3316 
  3317     // Memory handling of the sequence after the action
  3318     switch (mem) {
  3319         .free => self.keyboard.sequence_queued.clearAndFree(self.alloc),
  3320         .retain => self.keyboard.sequence_queued.clearRetainingCapacity(),
  3321     }
  3322 }
  3323 
  3324 /// Encodes the key event into a write request. The write request will
  3325 /// always copy or allocate so the caller can safely free the event.
  3326 fn encodeKey(
  3327     self: *Surface,
  3328     event: input.KeyEvent,
  3329     insp_ev: ?*inspectorpkg.KeyEvent,
  3330 ) !?termio.Message.WriteReq {
  3331     const write_req: termio.Message.WriteReq = req: {
  3332         // Build our encoding options, which requires the lock.
  3333         const encoding_opts = self.encodeKeyOpts();
  3334 
  3335         // Try to write the input into a small array. This fits almost
  3336         // every scenario. Larger situations can happen due to long
  3337         // pre-edits.
  3338         var data: termio.Message.WriteReq.Small.Array = undefined;
  3339         var writer: std.Io.Writer = .fixed(&data);
  3340         if (input.key_encode.encode(
  3341             &writer,
  3342             event,
  3343             encoding_opts,
  3344         )) {
  3345             const written = writer.buffered();
  3346 
  3347             // Special-case: we did nothing.
  3348             if (written.len == 0) return null;
  3349 
  3350             break :req .{ .small = .{
  3351                 .data = data,
  3352                 .len = @intCast(written.len),
  3353             } };
  3354         } else |err| switch (err) {
  3355             // Means we need to allocate
  3356             error.WriteFailed => {},
  3357         }
  3358 
  3359         // We need to allocate. We allocate double the UTF-8 length
  3360         // or double the small array size, whichever is larger. That's
  3361         // a heuristic that should work. The only scenario I know while
  3362         // typing this where we don't have enough space is a long preedit,
  3363         // and in that case the size we need is exactly the UTF-8 length,
  3364         // so the double is being safe.
  3365         var alloc_writer: std.Io.Writer.Allocating = try .initCapacity(
  3366             self.alloc,
  3367             @max(event.utf8.len * 2, data.len * 2),
  3368         );
  3369         defer alloc_writer.deinit();
  3370 
  3371         try input.key_encode.encode(
  3372             &alloc_writer.writer,
  3373             event,
  3374             encoding_opts,
  3375         );
  3376         break :req .{ .alloc = .{
  3377             .alloc = self.alloc,
  3378             .data = try alloc_writer.toOwnedSlice(),
  3379         } };
  3380     };
  3381 
  3382     // Copy the encoded data into the inspector event if we have one.
  3383     // We do this before the mailbox because the IO thread could
  3384     // release the memory before we get a chance to copy it.
  3385     if (insp_ev) |ev| pty: {
  3386         const slice = write_req.slice();
  3387         const copy = self.alloc.alloc(u8, slice.len) catch |err| {
  3388             log.warn("error allocating pty data for inspector err={}", .{err});
  3389             break :pty;
  3390         };
  3391         errdefer self.alloc.free(copy);
  3392         @memcpy(copy, slice);
  3393         ev.pty = copy;
  3394     }
  3395 
  3396     return write_req;
  3397 }
  3398 
  3399 fn encodeKeyOpts(self: *const Surface) input.key_encode.Options {
  3400     self.renderer_state.mutex.lockUncancelable(global.io());
  3401     defer self.renderer_state.mutex.unlock(global.io());
  3402     const t = &self.io.terminal;
  3403 
  3404     var opts: input.key_encode.Options = .fromTerminal(t);
  3405     if (comptime builtin.target.os.tag != .macos) return opts;
  3406 
  3407     opts.macos_option_as_alt = self.config.macos_option_as_alt orelse detect: {
  3408         // If we don't have alt pressed, it doesn't matter what this
  3409         // config is so we can just say "false" and break out and avoid
  3410         // more expensive checks below.
  3411         if (!self.mouse.mods.alt) break :detect .false;
  3412 
  3413         // Alt is pressed, we're on macOS. We break some encapsulation
  3414         // here and assume libghostty for ease...
  3415         break :detect self.rt_app.keyboardLayout().detectOptionAsAlt();
  3416     };
  3417 
  3418     return opts;
  3419 }

  3421 /// Sends text as-is to the terminal without triggering any keyboard
  3422 /// protocol. This will treat the input text as if it was pasted

  3430

  3432 }

n24 src/Surface.zig:3813–3905 DIU
  3813 fn mouseReport(
  3814     self: *Surface,
  3815     button: ?input.MouseButton,
  3816     action: input.MouseAction,
  3817     mods: input.Mods,
  3818     pos: apprt.CursorPos,
  3819 ) void {
  3820     // Mouse reporting must be enabled by both config and terminal state
  3821     assert(self.config.mouse_reporting);
  3822     assert(self.io.terminal.flags.mouse_event != .none);
  3823 
  3824     // Build our encoding options.
  3825     const encoding_opts: input.mouse_encode.Options = opts: {
  3826         // Terminal and size state.
  3827         var opts: input.mouse_encode.Options = .fromTerminal(
  3828             &self.io.terminal,
  3829             self.size,
  3830         );
  3831 
  3832         // Whether any button is pressed at all.
  3833         opts.any_button_pressed = pressed: {
  3834             for (self.mouse.click_state) |state| {
  3835                 if (state != .release) break :pressed true;
  3836             }
  3837 
  3838             break :pressed false;
  3839         };
  3840 
  3841         // Keep track of our last reported viewport cell for event
  3842         // deduplication.
  3843         opts.last_cell = &self.mouse.event_point;
  3844 
  3845         break :opts opts;
  3846     };
  3847 
  3848     var data: termio.Message.WriteReq.Small.Array = undefined;
  3849     var writer: std.Io.Writer = .fixed(&data);
  3850     input.mouse_encode.encode(&writer, .{
  3851         .button = button,
  3852         .action = action,
  3853         .mods = mods,
  3854         .pos = .{
  3855             .x = pos.x,
  3856             .y = pos.y,
  3857         },
  3858     }, encoding_opts) catch |err| switch (err) {
  3859         error.WriteFailed => {
  3860             // This should never happen since mouse events should never
  3861             // be able to overflow the size of our small array. But if it
  3862             // does, let's log it and return. No need to crash upstreams.
  3863             // In the future we may want to fall back to allocation.
  3864             log.warn("failed to encode mouse event err={}", .{err});
  3865             return;
  3866         },
  3867     };
  3868     const written = writer.buffered();
  3869     if (written.len == 0) return;
  3870 
  3871     self.queueIo(.{ .write_small = .{
  3872         .data = data,
  3873         .len = @intCast(written.len),
  3874     } }, .locked);
  3875 }
  3876 
  3877 /// Returns true if the shift modifier is allowed to be captured by modifier
  3878 /// events. It is up to the caller to still verify it is a situation in which
  3879 /// shift capture makes sense (i.e. left button, mouse click, etc.)
  3880 fn mouseShiftCapture(self: *const Surface, lock: bool) bool {
  3881     // Handle our never/always case where we don't need a lock.
  3882     switch (self.config.mouse_shift_capture) {
  3883         .never => return false,
  3884         .always => return true,
  3885         .false, .true => {},
  3886     }
  3887 
  3888     if (lock) self.renderer_state.mutex.lockUncancelable(global.io());
  3889     defer if (lock) self.renderer_state.mutex.unlock(global.io());
  3890 
  3891     // If the terminal explicitly requests it then we always allow it
  3892     // since we processed never/always at this point.
  3893     switch (self.io.terminal.flags.mouse_shift_capture) {
  3894         .false => return false,
  3895         .true => return true,
  3896         .null => {},
  3897     }
  3898 
  3899     // Otherwise, go with the user's preference
  3900     return switch (self.config.mouse_shift_capture) {
  3901         .false => false,
  3902         .true => true,
  3903         .never, .always => unreachable, // handled earlier
  3904     };
  3905 }

n24 src/Surface.zig:4057–4094 IU
  4057     // Report mouse events if enabled
  4058     {
  4059         self.renderer_state.mutex.lockUncancelable(global.io());
  4060         defer self.renderer_state.mutex.unlock(global.io());
  4061         if (self.isMouseReporting()) report: {

  4079                 .press => .press,
  4080                 .release => .release,

  4084                 button,
  4085                 report_action,
  4086                 self.mouse.mods,
  4087                 pos,
  4088             );
  4089

  4091             // selection or highlighting.
  4092             return true;

  4778     // Do a mouse report
  4779     if (self.isMouseReporting()) report: {
  4780         // Shift overrides mouse "grabbing" in the window, taken from Kitty.
  4781         // This only applies if there is a mouse button pressed so that

n24 src/Surface.zig:4941–5819 DIU
  4941 pub fn performBindingAction(self: *Surface, action: input.Binding.Action) !bool {
  4942     // Forward app-scoped actions to the app. Some app-scoped actions are
  4943     // special-cased here because they do some special things when performed
  4944     // from the surface.
  4945     if (action.scoped(.app)) |app_action| {
  4946         switch (app_action) {
  4947             .new_window => try self.app.newWindow(
  4948                 self.rt_app,
  4949                 .{ .parent = self },
  4950             ),
  4951 
  4952             // Undo and redo both support both surface and app targeting.
  4953             // If we are triggering on a surface then we perform the
  4954             // action with the surface target.
  4955             .undo => return try self.rt_app.performAction(
  4956                 .{ .surface = self },
  4957                 .undo,
  4958                 {},
  4959             ),
  4960 
  4961             .redo => return try self.rt_app.performAction(
  4962                 .{ .surface = self },
  4963                 .redo,
  4964                 {},
  4965             ),
  4966 
  4967             else => try self.app.performAction(
  4968                 self.rt_app,
  4969                 action.scoped(.app).?,
  4970             ),
  4971         }
  4972         return true;
  4973     }
  4974 
  4975     switch (action.scoped(.surface).?) {
  4976         .csi, .esc => |data| {
  4977             // We need to send the CSI/ESC sequence as a single write request.
  4978             // If you split it across two then the shell can interpret it
  4979             // as two literals.
  4980             var buf: [128]u8 = undefined;
  4981             const full_data = switch (action) {
  4982                 .csi => try std.mem.print(&buf, "\x1b[{s}", .{data}),
  4983                 .esc => try std.mem.print(&buf, "\x1b{s}", .{data}),
  4984                 else => unreachable,
  4985             };
  4986             self.queueIo(try termio.Message.writeReq(
  4987                 self.alloc,
  4988                 full_data,
  4989             ), .unlocked);
  4990 
  4991             // CSI/ESC triggers a scroll.
  4992             {
  4993                 self.renderer_state.mutex.lockUncancelable(global.io());
  4994                 defer self.renderer_state.mutex.unlock(global.io());
  4995                 self.scrollToBottom() catch |err| {
  4996                     log.warn("error scrolling to bottom err={}", .{err});
  4997                 };
  4998             }
  4999         },
  5000 
  5001         .text => |data| {
  5002             var stack_buf: [256]u8 = undefined;
  5003             var stack: std.heap.BufferFirstAllocator = .init(&stack_buf, self.alloc);
  5004             const alloc = stack.allocator();
  5005             const buf = try alloc.alloc(u8, data.len);
  5006             defer alloc.free(buf);
  5007             const text = configpkg.string.parse(buf, data) catch |err| {
  5008                 log.warn(
  5009                     "error parsing text binding text={s} err={}",
  5010                     .{ data, err },
  5011                 );
  5012                 return true;
  5013             };
  5014             self.queueIo(try termio.Message.writeReq(
  5015                 self.alloc,
  5016                 text,
  5017             ), .unlocked);
  5018 
  5019             // Text triggers a scroll.
  5020             {
  5021                 self.renderer_state.mutex.lockUncancelable(global.io());
  5022                 defer self.renderer_state.mutex.unlock(global.io());
  5023                 self.scrollToBottom() catch |err| {
  5024                     log.warn("error scrolling to bottom err={}", .{err});
  5025                 };
  5026             }
  5027         },
  5028 
  5029         .cursor_key => |ck| {
  5030             // We send a different sequence depending on if we're
  5031             // in cursor keys mode. We're in "normal" mode if cursor
  5032             // keys mode is NOT set.
  5033             const normal = normal: {
  5034                 self.renderer_state.mutex.lockUncancelable(global.io());
  5035                 defer self.renderer_state.mutex.unlock(global.io());
  5036 
  5037                 // With the lock held, we must scroll to the bottom.
  5038                 // We always scroll to the bottom for these inputs.
  5039                 self.scrollToBottom() catch |err| {
  5040                     log.warn("error scrolling to bottom err={}", .{err});
  5041                 };
  5042 
  5043                 break :normal !self.io.terminal.modes.get(.cursor_keys);
  5044             };
  5045 
  5046             if (normal) {
  5047                 self.queueIo(.{ .write_stable = ck.normal }, .unlocked);
  5048             } else {
  5049                 self.queueIo(.{ .write_stable = ck.application }, .unlocked);
  5050             }
  5051         },
  5052 
  5053         .reset => {
  5054             self.renderer_state.mutex.lockUncancelable(global.io());
  5055             defer self.renderer_state.mutex.unlock(global.io());
  5056             self.renderer_state.terminal.fullReset();
  5057         },
  5058 
  5059         .start_search => {
  5060             // To save resources, we don't actually start a search here,
  5061             // we just notify the apprt. The real thread will start when
  5062             // the first needles are set.
  5063             return try self.rt_app.performAction(
  5064                 .{ .surface = self },
  5065                 .start_search,
  5066                 .{ .needle = "" },
  5067             );
  5068         },
  5069 
  5070         .search_selection => {
  5071             const selection = try self.selectionString(self.alloc) orelse return false;
  5072             defer self.alloc.free(selection);
  5073             return try self.rt_app.performAction(
  5074                 .{ .surface = self },
  5075                 .start_search,
  5076                 .{ .needle = selection },
  5077             );
  5078         },
  5079 
  5080         .end_search => {
  5081             // We only return that this was performed if we actually
  5082             // stopped a search, but we also send the apprt end_search so
  5083             // that GUIs can clean up stale stuff.
  5084             const performed = self.search != null;
  5085 
  5086             if (self.search) |*s| {
  5087                 s.deinit();
  5088                 self.search = null;
  5089             }
  5090 
  5091             _ = try self.rt_app.performAction(
  5092                 .{ .surface = self },
  5093                 .end_search,
  5094                 {},
  5095             );
  5096 
  5097             return performed;
  5098         },
  5099 
  5100         .search => |text| search: {
  5101             const s: *Search = if (self.search) |*s| s else init: {
  5102                 // If we're stopping the search and we had no prior search,
  5103                 // then there is nothing to do.
  5104                 if (text.len == 0) return false;
  5105 
  5106                 // We need to assign directly to self.search because we need
  5107                 // a stable pointer back to the thread state.
  5108                 self.search = .{
  5109                     .state = try .init(self.alloc, .{
  5110                         .mutex = self.renderer_state.mutex,
  5111                         .terminal = self.renderer_state.terminal,
  5112                         .event_cb = &searchCallback,
  5113                         .event_userdata = self,
  5114                     }),
  5115                     .thread = undefined,
  5116                 };
  5117                 const s: *Search = &self.search.?;
  5118                 errdefer s.state.deinit();
  5119 
  5120                 s.thread = try .spawn(
  5121                     .{},
  5122                     terminal.search.Thread.threadMain,
  5123                     .{&s.state},
  5124                 );
  5125                 s.thread.setName(global.io(), "search") catch {};
  5126 
  5127                 break :init s;
  5128             };
  5129 
  5130             // Zero-length text means stop searching.
  5131             if (text.len == 0) {
  5132                 s.deinit();
  5133                 self.search = null;
  5134                 break :search;
  5135             }
  5136 
  5137             _ = s.state.mailbox.push(
  5138                 global.io(),
  5139                 .{ .change_needle = try .init(
  5140                     self.alloc,
  5141                     text,
  5142                 ) },
  5143                 .forever,
  5144             );
  5145             s.state.wakeup.notify() catch {};
  5146         },
  5147 
  5148         .navigate_search => |nav| {
  5149             const s: *Search = if (self.search) |*s| s else return false;
  5150             _ = s.state.mailbox.push(
  5151                 global.io(),
  5152                 .{ .select = switch (nav) {
  5153                     .next => .next,
  5154                     .previous => .prev,
  5155                 } },
  5156                 .forever,
  5157             );
  5158             s.state.wakeup.notify() catch {};
  5159         },
  5160 
  5161         .copy_to_clipboard => |format| {
  5162             self.renderer_state.mutex.lockUncancelable(global.io());
  5163             defer self.renderer_state.mutex.unlock(global.io());
  5164 
  5165             if (self.io.terminal.screens.active.selection) |sel| {
  5166                 try self.copySelectionToClipboards(
  5167                     sel,
  5168                     &.{.standard},
  5169                     format,
  5170                 );
  5171 
  5172                 // Clear the selection if configured to do so.
  5173                 if (self.config.selection_clear_on_copy) {
  5174                     if (self.setSelection(null)) {
  5175                         self.queueRender() catch |err| {
  5176                             log.warn("failed to queue render after clear selection err={}", .{err});
  5177                         };
  5178                     } else |err| {
  5179                         log.warn("failed to clear selection after copy err={}", .{err});
  5180                     }
  5181                 }
  5182 
  5183                 return true;
  5184             }
  5185 
  5186             return false;
  5187         },
  5188 
  5189         .copy_url_to_clipboard => {
  5190             // If the mouse isn't over a link, nothing we can do.
  5191             if (!self.mouse.over_link) return false;
  5192             const pos = try self.rt_surface.getCursorPos();
  5193 
  5194             self.renderer_state.mutex.lockUncancelable(global.io());
  5195             defer self.renderer_state.mutex.unlock(global.io());
  5196             if (try self.linkAtPos(pos)) |link_info| {
  5197                 const url_text = switch (link_info.action) {
  5198                     .open => url_text: {
  5199                         // For regex links, get the text from selection
  5200                         break :url_text (self.io.terminal.screens.active.selectionString(self.alloc, .{
  5201                             .sel = link_info.selection,
  5202                             .trim = self.config.clipboard_trim_trailing_spaces,
  5203                         })) catch |err| {
  5204                             log.err("error reading url string err={}", .{err});
  5205                             return false;
  5206                         };
  5207                     },
  5208 
  5209                     ._open_osc8 => url_text: {
  5210                         // For OSC8 links, get the URI directly from hyperlink data
  5211                         const uri = self.osc8URI(link_info.selection.start()) orelse {
  5212                             log.warn("failed to get URI for OSC8 hyperlink", .{});
  5213                             return false;
  5214                         };
  5215                         break :url_text try self.alloc.dupeSentinel(u8, uri, 0);
  5216                     },
  5217                 };
  5218                 defer self.alloc.free(url_text);
  5219 
  5220                 self.rt_surface.setClipboard(.standard, &.{.{
  5221                     .mime = "text/plain",
  5222                     .data = url_text,
  5223                 }}, false) catch |err| {
  5224                     log.err("error copying url to clipboard err={}", .{err});
  5225                     return false;
  5226                 };
  5227 
  5228                 return true;
  5229             }
  5230 
  5231             return false;
  5232         },
  5233 
  5234         .copy_title_to_clipboard => return try self.rt_app.performAction(
  5235             .{ .surface = self },
  5236             .copy_title_to_clipboard,
  5237             {},
  5238         ),
  5239

  5250         .increase_font_size => |delta| {
  5251             // Max delta is somewhat arbitrary.
  5252             const clamped_delta = @max(0, @min(255, delta));
  5253 
  5254             log.debug("increase font size={}", .{clamped_delta});
  5255 
  5256             // Max point size is somewhat arbitrary.
  5257             var size = self.font_size;
  5258             size.points = @min(size.points + clamped_delta, 255);
  5259             try self.setFontSize(size);
  5260 
  5261             // Mark that we manually adjusted the font size
  5262             self.font_size_adjusted = true;
  5263         },
  5264 
  5265         .decrease_font_size => |delta| {
  5266             // Max delta is somewhat arbitrary.
  5267             const clamped_delta = @max(0, @min(255, delta));
  5268 
  5269             log.debug("decrease font size={}", .{clamped_delta});
  5270 
  5271             var size = self.font_size;
  5272             size.points = @max(1, size.points - clamped_delta);
  5273             try self.setFontSize(size);
  5274 
  5275             // Mark that we manually adjusted the font size
  5276             self.font_size_adjusted = true;
  5277         },
  5278 
  5279         .reset_font_size => {
  5280             log.debug("reset font size", .{});
  5281 
  5282             var size = self.font_size;
  5283             size.points = self.config.original_font_size;
  5284             try self.setFontSize(size);
  5285 
  5286             // Reset font size also resets the manual adjustment state
  5287             self.font_size_adjusted = false;
  5288         },
  5289 
  5290         .set_font_size => |points| {
  5291             log.debug("set font size={d}", .{points});
  5292 
  5293             var size = self.font_size;
  5294             size.points = std.math.clamp(points, 1.0, 255.0);
  5295             try self.setFontSize(size);
  5296 
  5297             // Mark that we manually adjusted the font size
  5298             self.font_size_adjusted = true;
  5299         },

  5349         .clear_screen => {
  5350             // This is a duplicate of some of the logic in termio.clearScreen
  5351             // but we need to do this here so we can know the answer before
  5352             // we send the message. If the currently active screen is on the
  5353             // alternate screen then clear screen does nothing so we want to
  5354             // return false so the keybind can be unconsumed.
  5355             {
  5356                 self.renderer_state.mutex.lockUncancelable(global.io());
  5357                 defer self.renderer_state.mutex.unlock(global.io());
  5358                 if (self.io.terminal.screens.active_key == .alternate) return false;
  5359             }
  5360 
  5361             self.queueIo(.{
  5362                 .clear_screen = .{ .history = true },
  5363             }, .unlocked);
  5364         },

  5664 
  5665         inline .activate_key_table,
  5666         .activate_key_table_once,
  5667         => |name, tag| {
  5668             // Look up the table in our config
  5669             const set = self.config.keybind.tables.getPtr(name) orelse {
  5670                 log.debug("key table not found: {s}", .{name});
  5671                 return false;
  5672             };
  5673 
  5674             // If this is the same table as is currently active, then
  5675             // do nothing.
  5676             if (self.keyboard.table_stack.items.len > 0) {
  5677                 const items = self.keyboard.table_stack.items;
  5678                 const active = items[items.len - 1].set;
  5679                 if (active == set) {
  5680                     log.debug("ignoring duplicate activate table: {s}", .{name});
  5681                     return false;
  5682                 }
  5683             }
  5684 
  5685             // If we're already at the max, ignore it.
  5686             if (self.keyboard.table_stack.items.len >= max_active_key_tables) {
  5687                 log.info(
  5688                     "ignoring activate table, max depth reached: {s}",
  5689                     .{name},
  5690                 );
  5691                 return false;
  5692             }
  5693 
  5694             // Add the table to the stack.
  5695             try self.keyboard.table_stack.append(self.alloc, .{
  5696                 .set = set,
  5697                 .once = tag == .activate_key_table_once,
  5698             });
  5699 
  5700             // Notify the UI.
  5701             _ = self.rt_app.performAction(
  5702                 .{ .surface = self },
  5703                 .key_table,
  5704                 .{ .activate = name },
  5705             ) catch |err| {
  5706                 log.warn(
  5707                     "failed to notify app of key table err={}",
  5708                     .{err},
  5709                 );
  5710             };
  5711 
  5712             log.debug("key table activated: {s}", .{name});
  5713         },
  5714 
  5715         .deactivate_key_table => {
  5716             switch (self.keyboard.table_stack.items.len) {
  5717                 // No key table active. This does nothing.
  5718                 0 => return false,
  5719 
  5720                 // Final key table active, clear our state.
  5721                 1 => self.keyboard.table_stack.clearAndFree(self.alloc),
  5722 
  5723                 // Restore the prior key table. We don't free any memory in
  5724                 // this case because we assume it will be freed later when
  5725                 // we finish our key table.
  5726                 else => _ = self.keyboard.table_stack.pop(),
  5727             }
  5728 
  5729             // Notify the UI.
  5730             _ = self.rt_app.performAction(
  5731                 .{ .surface = self },
  5732                 .key_table,
  5733                 .deactivate,
  5734             ) catch |err| {
  5735                 log.warn(
  5736                     "failed to notify app of key table err={}",
  5737                     .{err},
  5738                 );
  5739             };
  5740         },
  5741 
  5742         .deactivate_all_key_tables => {
  5743             return try self.deactivateAllKeyTables();
  5744         },
  5745 
  5746         .end_key_sequence => {
  5747             // End the key sequence and flush queued keys to the terminal,
  5748             // but don't encode the key that triggered this action. This
  5749             // will do that because leaf keys (keys with bindings) aren't
  5750             // in the queued encoding list.
  5751             self.endKeySequence(.flush, .retain);
  5752         },
  5753 
  5754         .crash => |location| switch (location) {
  5755             .main => @panic("crash binding action, crashing intentionally"),
  5756 
  5757             .render => {
  5758                 _ = self.renderer_thread.mailbox.push(global.io(), .{ .crash = {} }, .{ .forever = {} });
  5759                 self.queueRender() catch |err| {
  5760                     // Not a big deal if this fails.
  5761                     log.warn("failed to notify renderer of crash message err={}", .{err});
  5762                 };
  5763             },
  5764 
  5765             .io => self.queueIo(.{ .crash = {} }, .unlocked),
  5766         },
  5767 
  5768         .adjust_selection => |direction| {
  5769             self.renderer_state.mutex.lockUncancelable(global.io());
  5770             defer self.renderer_state.mutex.unlock(global.io());
  5771 
  5772             const screen: *terminal.Screen = self.io.terminal.screens.active;
  5773             const sel = if (screen.selection) |*sel| sel else {
  5774                 // If we don't have a selection we do not perform this
  5775                 // action, allowing the keybind to fall through to the
  5776                 // terminal.
  5777                 return false;
  5778             };
  5779             sel.adjust(screen, switch (direction) {
  5780                 .left => .left,
  5781                 .right => .right,
  5782                 .up => .up,
  5783                 .down => .down,
  5784                 .page_up => .page_up,
  5785                 .page_down => .page_down,
  5786                 .home => .home,
  5787                 .end => .end,
  5788                 .beginning_of_line => .beginning_of_line,
  5789                 .end_of_line => .end_of_line,
  5790             });
  5791 
  5792             // If the selection endpoint is outside of the current viewpoint,
  5793             // scroll it in to view. Note we always specifically use sel.end
  5794             // because that is what adjust modifies.
  5795             scroll: {
  5796                 const viewport_tl = screen.pages.getTopLeft(.viewport);
  5797                 const viewport_br = screen.pages.getBottomRight(.viewport).?;
  5798                 if (sel.end().isBetween(viewport_tl, viewport_br))
  5799                     break :scroll;
  5800 
  5801                 // Our end point is not within the viewport. If the end
  5802                 // point is after the br then we need to adjust the end so
  5803                 // that it is at the bottom right of the viewport.
  5804                 const target = if (sel.end().before(viewport_tl))
  5805                     sel.end()
  5806                 else
  5807                     sel.end().up(screen.pages.rows - 1) orelse sel.end();
  5808 
  5809                 screen.scroll(.{ .pin = target });
  5810             }
  5811 
  5812             // Queue a render so its shown
  5813             screen.dirty.selection = true;
  5814             try self.queueRender();
  5815         },
  5816     }
  5817 
  5818     return true;
  5819 }
