n25 src/App.zig:486–529 DIU
   486 pub fn performAllChainedAction(
   487     self: *App,
   488     rt_app: *apprt.App,
   489     actions: []const input.Binding.Action,
   490 ) void {
   491     for (actions) |action| {
   492         self.performAllAction(rt_app, action) catch |err| {
   493             log.warn("error performing chained action action={s} err={}", .{
   494                 @tagName(action),
   495                 err,
   496             });
   497         };
   498     }
   499 }
   500 
   501 /// Perform an app-wide binding action. If the action is surface-specific
   502 /// then it will be performed on all surfaces. To perform only app-scoped
   503 /// actions, use performAction.
   504 pub fn performAllAction(
   505     self: *App,
   506     rt_app: *apprt.App,
   507     action: input.Binding.Action,
   508 ) !void {
   509     switch (action.scope()) {
   510         // App-scoped actions are handled by the app so that they aren't
   511         // repeated for each surface (since each surface forwards
   512         // app-scoped actions back up).
   513         .app => try self.performAction(
   514             rt_app,
   515             action.scoped(.app).?, // asserted through the scope match
   516         ),
   517 
   518         // Surface-scoped actions are performed on all surfaces. Errors
   519         // are logged but processing continues.
   520         .surface => for (self.surfaces.items) |surface| {
   521             _ = surface.core().performBindingAction(action) catch |err| {
   522                 log.warn("error performing binding action on surface id={x} err={}", .{
   523                     surface.core().id,
   524                     err,
   525                 });
   526             };
   527         },
   528     }
   529 }

n25 src/Surface.zig:2790–3219 DIU
  2790 /// Called for any key events. This handles keybindings, encoding and
  2791 /// sending to the terminal, etc.
  2792 pub fn keyCallback(
  2793     self: *Surface,
  2794     event_orig: input.KeyEvent,
  2795 ) !InputEffect {
  2796     // log.warn("text keyCallback event={}", .{event_orig});
  2797 
  2798     // Apply key remappings to transform modifiers before any processing.
  2799     // This allows users to remap modifier keys at the app level.
  2800     var event = event_orig;
  2801     if (self.config.key_remaps.isRemapped(event_orig.mods)) {
  2802         event.mods = self.config.key_remaps.apply(event_orig.mods);
  2803     }
  2804 
  2805     // Crash metadata in case we crash in here
  2806     crash.sentry.thread_state = self.crashThreadState();
  2807     defer crash.sentry.thread_state = null;
  2808 
  2809     // Setup our inspector event if we have an inspector.
  2810     var insp_ev: ?inspectorpkg.KeyEvent = if (self.inspector != null) ev: {
  2811         var copy = event;
  2812         copy.utf8 = "";
  2813         if (event.utf8.len > 0) copy.utf8 = try self.alloc.dupe(u8, event.utf8);
  2814         break :ev .{ .event = copy };
  2815     } else null;
  2816 
  2817     // When we're done processing, we always want to add the event to
  2818     // the inspector.
  2819     defer if (insp_ev) |ev| ev: {
  2820         // We have to check for the inspector again because our keybinding
  2821         // might close it.
  2822         const insp = self.inspector orelse {
  2823             ev.deinit(self.alloc);
  2824             break :ev;
  2825         };
  2826 
  2827         if (insp.recordKeyEvent(self.alloc, ev)) {
  2828             self.queueRender() catch {};
  2829         } else |err| {
  2830             log.warn("error adding key event to inspector err={}", .{err});
  2831         }
  2832     };
  2833 
  2834     // Handle keybindings first. We need to handle this on all events
  2835     // (press, repeat, release) because a press may perform a binding but
  2836     // a release should not encode if we consumed the press.
  2837     if (try self.maybeHandleBinding(
  2838         event,
  2839         if (insp_ev) |*ev| ev else null,
  2840     )) |v| return v;
  2841     // If we allow KAM and KAM is enabled then we do nothing.
  2842     if (self.config.vt_kam_allowed) {
  2843         self.renderer_state.mutex.lockUncancelable(global.io());
  2844         defer self.renderer_state.mutex.unlock(global.io());
  2845         if (self.io.terminal.modes.get(.disable_keyboard)) return .consumed;
  2846     }
  2847 
  2848     // If this input event has text, then we hide the mouse if configured.
  2849     // We only do this on pressed events to avoid hiding the mouse when we
  2850     // change focus due to a keybinding (i.e. switching tabs).
  2851     if (self.config.mouse_hide_while_typing and
  2852         event.action == .press and
  2853         !self.mouse.hidden and
  2854         event.utf8.len > 0)
  2855     {
  2856         self.hideMouse();
  2857     }
  2858 
  2859     // If our mouse modifiers change we may need to change our
  2860     // link highlight state.
  2861     if (!self.mouse.mods.equal(event.mods)) mouse_mods: {
  2862         // Update our modifiers, this will update mouse mods too
  2863         self.modsChanged(event.mods);
  2864 
  2865         // We only refresh links if
  2866         // 1. mouse reporting is off
  2867         // OR
  2868         // 2. mouse reporting is on and we are not reporting shift to the terminal
  2869         if (self.io.terminal.flags.mouse_event == .none or
  2870             (self.mouse.mods.shift and !self.mouseShiftCapture(false)))
  2871         {
  2872             // Refresh our link state
  2873             const pos = self.rt_surface.getCursorPos() catch break :mouse_mods;
  2874             self.renderer_state.mutex.lockUncancelable(global.io());
  2875             defer self.renderer_state.mutex.unlock(global.io());
  2876             self.mouseRefreshLinks(
  2877                 pos,
  2878                 self.posToViewport(pos.x, pos.y),
  2879                 self.mouse.over_link,
  2880             ) catch |err| {
  2881                 log.warn("failed to refresh links err={}", .{err});
  2882                 break :mouse_mods;
  2883             };
  2884         } else if (self.io.terminal.flags.mouse_event != .none and !self.mouse.mods.shift) {
  2885             // If we have mouse reports on and we don't have shift pressed, we reset state
  2886             _ = try self.rt_app.performAction(
  2887                 .{ .surface = self },
  2888                 .mouse_shape,
  2889                 self.io.terminal.mouse_shape,
  2890             );
  2891             _ = try self.rt_app.performAction(
  2892                 .{ .surface = self },
  2893                 .mouse_over_link,
  2894                 .{ .url = "" },
  2895             );
  2896             try self.queueRender();
  2897         }
  2898     }
  2899 
  2900     // Process the cursor state logic. This will update the cursor shape if
  2901     // needed, depending on the key state.
  2902     if ((SurfaceMouse{
  2903         .physical_key = event.key,
  2904         .mouse_event = self.io.terminal.flags.mouse_event,
  2905         .mouse_shape = self.io.terminal.mouse_shape,
  2906         .mods = self.mouse.mods,
  2907         .over_link = self.mouse.over_link,
  2908         .hidden = self.mouse.hidden,
  2909     }).keyToMouseShape()) |shape| _ = try self.rt_app.performAction(
  2910         .{ .surface = self },
  2911         .mouse_shape,
  2912         shape,
  2913     );
  2914 
  2915     // We've processed a key event that produced some data so we want to
  2916     // track the last pressed key.
  2917     self.pressed_key = event: {
  2918         // We need to unset the allocated fields that will become invalid
  2919         var copy = event;
  2920         copy.utf8 = "";
  2921 
  2922         // If we have a previous pressed key and we're releasing it
  2923         // then we set it to invalid to prevent repeating the release event.
  2924         if (event.action == .release) {
  2925             // if we didn't have a previous event and this is a release
  2926             // event then we just want to set it to null.
  2927             const prev = self.pressed_key orelse break :event null;
  2928             if (prev.key == copy.key) copy.key = .unidentified;
  2929         }
  2930 
  2931         // If our key is invalid and we have no mods, then we're done!
  2932         // This helps catch the state that we naturally released all keys.
  2933         if (copy.key == .unidentified and copy.mods.empty()) break :event null;
  2934 
  2935         break :event copy;
  2936     };
  2937 
  2938     // Encode and send our key. If we didn't encode anything, then we
  2939     // return the effect as ignored.
  2940     if (try self.encodeKey(
  2941         event,
  2942         if (insp_ev) |*ev| ev else null,
  2943     )) |write_req| {
  2944         // If our process is exited and we press a key that results in
  2945         // an encoded value, we close the surface. We want to eventually
  2946         // move this behavior to the apprt probably.
  2947         if (self.child_exited) {
  2948             write_req.deinit();
  2949             self.close();
  2950             return .closed;
  2951         }
  2952 
  2953         self.queueIo(switch (write_req) {
  2954             .small => |v| .{ .write_small = v },
  2955             .stable => |v| .{ .write_stable = v },
  2956             .alloc => |v| .{ .write_alloc = v },
  2957         }, .unlocked);
  2958     } else {
  2959         // No valid request means that we didn't encode anything.
  2960         return .ignored;
  2961     }
  2962 
  2963     // If our event is any keypress that isn't a modifier and we generated
  2964     // some data to send to the pty, then we move the viewport down to the
  2965     // bottom. We also clear the selection for any key other then modifiers.
  2966     if (!event.key.modifier()) {
  2967         self.renderer_state.mutex.lockUncancelable(global.io());
  2968         defer self.renderer_state.mutex.unlock(global.io());
  2969 
  2970         if (self.config.selection_clear_on_typing or
  2971             event.key == .escape)
  2972         {
  2973             try self.setSelection(null);
  2974         }
  2975 
  2976         if (self.config.scroll_to_bottom.keystroke) self.io.terminal.scrollViewport(.bottom);
  2977 
  2978         try self.queueRender();
  2979     }
  2980 
  2981     return .consumed;
  2982 }
  2983 
  2984 /// Maybe handles a binding for a given event and if so returns the effect.
  2985 /// Returns null if the event is not handled in any way and processing should
  2986 /// continue.
  2987 fn maybeHandleBinding(
  2988     self: *Surface,
  2989     event: input.KeyEvent,
  2990     insp_ev: ?*inspectorpkg.KeyEvent,
  2991 ) !?InputEffect {
  2992     switch (event.action) {
  2993         // Release events never trigger a binding but we need to check if
  2994         // we consumed the press event so we don't encode the release.
  2995         .release => {
  2996             if (self.keyboard.last_trigger) |last| {
  2997                 if (last == event.bindingHash()) {
  2998                     // We don't reset the last trigger on release because
  2999                     // an apprt may send multiple release events for a single
  3000                     // press event.
  3001                     return .consumed;
  3002                 }
  3003             }
  3004 
  3005             return null;
  3006         },
  3007 
  3008         // Carry on processing.
  3009         .press, .repeat => {},
  3010     }
  3011 
  3012     // Find an entry in the keybind set that matches our event.
  3013     const entry: input.Binding.Set.Entry = entry: {
  3014         // Handle key sequences first.
  3015         if (self.keyboard.sequence_set) |set| {
  3016             // Get our entry from the set for the given event.
  3017             if (set.getEvent(event)) |v| break :entry v;
  3018 
  3019             // No entry found. We need to encode everything up to this
  3020             // point and send to the pty since we're in a sequence.
  3021 
  3022             // We ignore modifiers so that nested sequences such as
  3023             // ctrl+a>ctrl+b>c work.
  3024             if (event.key.modifier()) return null;
  3025 
  3026             // If we have a catch-all of ignore, then we special case our
  3027             // invalid sequence handling to ignore it.
  3028             if (self.catchAllIsIgnore()) {
  3029                 self.endKeySequence(.drop, .retain);
  3030                 return .ignored;
  3031             }
  3032 
  3033             // Encode everything up to this point
  3034             self.endKeySequence(.flush, .retain);
  3035 
  3036             return null;
  3037         }
  3038 
  3039         // No currently active sequence, move on to tables. For tables,
  3040         // we search inner-most table to outer-most. The table stack does
  3041         // NOT include the root set.
  3042         const table_items = self.keyboard.table_stack.items;
  3043         if (table_items.len > 0) {
  3044             for (0..table_items.len) |i| {
  3045                 const rev_i: usize = table_items.len - 1 - i;
  3046                 const table = table_items[rev_i];
  3047                 if (table.set.getEvent(event)) |v| {
  3048                     // If this is a one-shot activation AND its the currently
  3049                     // active table, then we deactivate it after this.
  3050                     // Note: we may want to change the semantics here to
  3051                     // remove this table no matter where it is in the stack,
  3052                     // maybe.
  3053                     if (table.once and i == 0) _ = try self.performBindingAction(
  3054                         .deactivate_key_table,
  3055                     );
  3056 
  3057                     break :entry v;
  3058                 }
  3059             }
  3060         }
  3061 
  3062         // No table, use our default set
  3063         break :entry self.config.keybind.set.getEvent(event) orelse
  3064             return null;
  3065     };
  3066 
  3067     // Determine if this entry has an action or if its a leader key.
  3068     const leaf: input.Binding.Set.GenericLeaf = switch (entry.value_ptr.*) {
  3069         .leader => |set| {
  3070             // Store this event so that we can drain and encode on invalid.
  3071             // We don't need to cap this because it is naturally capped by
  3072             // the config validation.
  3073             if (try self.encodeKey(event, insp_ev)) |req| {
  3074                 self.keyboard.sequence_queued.append(self.alloc, req) catch |err| {
  3075                     req.deinit();
  3076                     return err;
  3077                 };
  3078             }
  3079 
  3080             // Setup the next set we'll look at only after all fallible work.
  3081             self.keyboard.sequence_set = set;
  3082 
  3083             // Start or continue our key sequence
  3084             _ = self.rt_app.performAction(
  3085                 .{ .surface = self },
  3086                 .key_sequence,
  3087                 .{ .trigger = entry.key_ptr.* },
  3088             ) catch |err| {
  3089                 log.warn(
  3090                     "failed to notify app of key sequence err={}",
  3091                     .{err},
  3092                 );
  3093             };
  3094 
  3095             return .consumed;
  3096         },
  3097 
  3098         inline .leaf, .leaf_chained => |leaf| leaf.generic(),
  3099     };
  3100 
  3101     // consumed determines if the input is consumed or if we continue
  3102     // encoding the key (if we have a key to encode).
  3103     const consumed = consumed: {
  3104         // If the consumed flag is explicitly set, then we are consumed.
  3105         if (leaf.flags.consumed) break :consumed true;
  3106 
  3107         // If the global or all flag is set, we always consume.
  3108         if (leaf.flags.global or leaf.flags.all) break :consumed true;
  3109 
  3110         break :consumed false;
  3111     };
  3112 
  3113     // We have an action, so at this point we're handling SOMETHING so
  3114     // we reset the last trigger to null. We only set this if we actually
  3115     // perform an action (below)
  3116     self.keyboard.last_trigger = null;
  3117 
  3118     // An action also always resets the sequence set.
  3119     self.keyboard.sequence_set = null;
  3120 
  3121     // Setup our actions
  3122     const actions = leaf.actionsSlice();
  3123 
  3124     // Attempt to perform the action
  3125     log.debug("key event binding flags={} action={any}", .{
  3126         leaf.flags,
  3127         actions,
  3128     });
  3129     const performed = performed: {
  3130         // If this is a global or all action, then we perform it on
  3131         // the app and it applies to every surface.
  3132         if (leaf.flags.global or leaf.flags.all) {
  3133             self.app.performAllChainedAction(
  3134                 self.rt_app,
  3135                 actions,
  3136             );
  3137 
  3138             // "All" actions are always performed since they are global.
  3139             break :performed true;
  3140         }
  3141 
  3142         // Perform each action. We are performed if ANY of the chained
  3143         // actions perform.
  3144         var performed: bool = false;
  3145         for (actions) |action| {
  3146             if (self.performBindingAction(action)) |v| {
  3147                 performed = performed or v;
  3148             } else |err| {
  3149                 log.info(
  3150                     "key binding action failed action={t} err={}",
  3151                     .{ action, err },
  3152                 );
  3153             }
  3154         }
  3155 
  3156         break :performed performed;
  3157     };
  3158 
  3159     if (performed) {
  3160         // If we performed an action and it was a closing action,
  3161         // our "self" pointer is not safe to use anymore so we need to
  3162         // just exit immediately.
  3163         for (actions) |action| if (closingAction(action)) {
  3164             log.debug("key binding is a closing binding, halting key event processing", .{});
  3165             return .closed;
  3166         };
  3167 
  3168         // If our action was "ignore" then we return the special input
  3169         // effect of "ignored".
  3170         for (actions) |action| if (action == .ignore) {
  3171             // If we're in a sequence, clear it.
  3172             self.endKeySequence(.drop, .retain);
  3173 
  3174             return .ignored;
  3175         };
  3176     }
  3177 
  3178     // If we have the performable flag and the action was not performed,
  3179     // then we act as though a binding didn't exist.
  3180     if (leaf.flags.performable and !performed) {
  3181         // If we're in a sequence, we treat this as if we pressed a key
  3182         // that doesn't exist in the sequence. Reset our sequence and flush
  3183         // any queued events.
  3184         self.endKeySequence(.flush, .retain);
  3185 
  3186         return null;
  3187     }
  3188 
  3189     // If we consume this event, then we are done. If we don't consume
  3190     // it, we processed the action but we still want to process our
  3191     // encodings, too.
  3192     if (consumed) {
  3193         // If we had queued events, we deinit them since we consumed
  3194         self.endKeySequence(.drop, .retain);
  3195 
  3196         // Store our last trigger so we don't encode the release event
  3197         self.keyboard.last_trigger = event.bindingHash();
  3198 
  3199         if (insp_ev) |ev| {
  3200             ev.binding = self.alloc.dupe(
  3201                 input.Binding.Action,
  3202                 actions,
  3203             ) catch |err| binding: {
  3204                 log.warn(
  3205                     "error allocating binding action for inspector err={}",
  3206                     .{err},
  3207                 );
  3208                 break :binding &.{};
  3209             };
  3210         }
  3211         return .consumed;
  3212     }
  3213 
  3214     // If we didn't perform OR we didn't consume, then we want to
  3215     // encode any queued events for a sequence.
  3216     self.endKeySequence(.flush, .retain);
  3217 
  3218     return null;
  3219 }

n25 src/Surface.zig:3245–3419 DIU
  3245 /// This checks if the current keybinding sets have a catch_all binding
  3246 /// with `ignore`. This is used to determine some special input cases.
  3247 fn catchAllIsIgnore(self: *Surface) bool {
  3248     // Get our catch all
  3249     const entry: input.Binding.Set.Entry = entry: {
  3250         const trigger: input.Binding.Trigger = .{ .key = .catch_all };
  3251 
  3252         const table_items = self.keyboard.table_stack.items;
  3253         for (0..table_items.len) |i| {
  3254             const rev_i: usize = table_items.len - 1 - i;
  3255             const entry = table_items[rev_i].set.get(trigger) orelse continue;
  3256             break :entry entry;
  3257         }
  3258 
  3259         break :entry self.config.keybind.set.get(trigger) orelse
  3260             return false;
  3261     };
  3262 
  3263     // We have a catch-all entry, see if its an ignore
  3264     return switch (entry.value_ptr.*) {
  3265         .leader => false,
  3266         .leaf => |leaf| leaf.action == .ignore,
  3267         .leaf_chained => |leaf| chained: for (leaf.actions.items) |action| {
  3268             if (action == .ignore) break :chained true;
  3269         } else false,
  3270     };
  3271 }
  3272 
  3273 const KeySequenceQueued = enum { flush, drop };
  3274 const KeySequenceMemory = enum { retain, free };
  3275 
  3276 /// End a key sequence. Safe to call if no key sequence is active.
  3277 ///
  3278 /// Action and mem determine the behavior of the queued inputs up to this
  3279 /// point.
  3280 fn endKeySequence(
  3281     self: *Surface,
  3282     action: KeySequenceQueued,
  3283     mem: KeySequenceMemory,
  3284 ) void {
  3285     // Notify apprt key sequence ended
  3286     _ = self.rt_app.performAction(
  3287         .{ .surface = self },
  3288         .key_sequence,
  3289         .end,
  3290     ) catch |err| {
  3291         log.warn(
  3292             "failed to notify app of key sequence end err={}",
  3293             .{err},
  3294         );
  3295     };
  3296 
  3297     // No matter what we clear our current sequence set. This restores
  3298     // the set we look at to the root set.
  3299     self.keyboard.sequence_set = null;
  3300 
  3301     // If we have no queued data, there is nothing else to do.
  3302     if (self.keyboard.sequence_queued.items.len == 0) return;
  3303 
  3304     // Run the proper action first
  3305     switch (action) {
  3306         .flush => for (self.keyboard.sequence_queued.items) |write_req| {
  3307             self.queueIo(switch (write_req) {
  3308                 .small => |v| .{ .write_small = v },
  3309                 .stable => |v| .{ .write_stable = v },
  3310                 .alloc => |v| .{ .write_alloc = v },
  3311             }, .unlocked);
  3312         },
  3313 
  3314         .drop => for (self.keyboard.sequence_queued.items) |req| req.deinit(),
  3315     }
  3316 
  3317     // Memory handling of the sequence after the action
  3318     switch (mem) {
  3319         .free => self.keyboard.sequence_queued.clearAndFree(self.alloc),
  3320         .retain => self.keyboard.sequence_queued.clearRetainingCapacity(),
  3321     }
  3322 }
  3323 
  3324 /// Encodes the key event into a write request. The write request will
  3325 /// always copy or allocate so the caller can safely free the event.
  3326 fn encodeKey(
  3327     self: *Surface,
  3328     event: input.KeyEvent,
  3329     insp_ev: ?*inspectorpkg.KeyEvent,
  3330 ) !?termio.Message.WriteReq {
  3331     const write_req: termio.Message.WriteReq = req: {
  3332         // Build our encoding options, which requires the lock.
  3333         const encoding_opts = self.encodeKeyOpts();
  3334 
  3335         // Try to write the input into a small array. This fits almost
  3336         // every scenario. Larger situations can happen due to long
  3337         // pre-edits.
  3338         var data: termio.Message.WriteReq.Small.Array = undefined;
  3339         var writer: std.Io.Writer = .fixed(&data);
  3340         if (input.key_encode.encode(
  3341             &writer,
  3342             event,
  3343             encoding_opts,
  3344         )) {
  3345             const written = writer.buffered();
  3346 
  3347             // Special-case: we did nothing.
  3348             if (written.len == 0) return null;
  3349 
  3350             break :req .{ .small = .{
  3351                 .data = data,
  3352                 .len = @intCast(written.len),
  3353             } };
  3354         } else |err| switch (err) {
  3355             // Means we need to allocate
  3356             error.WriteFailed => {},
  3357         }
  3358 
  3359         // We need to allocate. We allocate double the UTF-8 length
  3360         // or double the small array size, whichever is larger. That's
  3361         // a heuristic that should work. The only scenario I know while
  3362         // typing this where we don't have enough space is a long preedit,
  3363         // and in that case the size we need is exactly the UTF-8 length,
  3364         // so the double is being safe.
  3365         var alloc_writer: std.Io.Writer.Allocating = try .initCapacity(
  3366             self.alloc,
  3367             @max(event.utf8.len * 2, data.len * 2),
  3368         );
  3369         defer alloc_writer.deinit();
  3370 
  3371         try input.key_encode.encode(
  3372             &alloc_writer.writer,
  3373             event,
  3374             encoding_opts,
  3375         );
  3376         break :req .{ .alloc = .{
  3377             .alloc = self.alloc,
  3378             .data = try alloc_writer.toOwnedSlice(),
  3379         } };
  3380     };
  3381 
  3382     // Copy the encoded data into the inspector event if we have one.
  3383     // We do this before the mailbox because the IO thread could
  3384     // release the memory before we get a chance to copy it.
  3385     if (insp_ev) |ev| pty: {
  3386         const slice = write_req.slice();
  3387         const copy = self.alloc.alloc(u8, slice.len) catch |err| {
  3388             log.warn("error allocating pty data for inspector err={}", .{err});
  3389             break :pty;
  3390         };
  3391         errdefer self.alloc.free(copy);
  3392         @memcpy(copy, slice);
  3393         ev.pty = copy;
  3394     }
  3395 
  3396     return write_req;
  3397 }
  3398 
  3399 fn encodeKeyOpts(self: *const Surface) input.key_encode.Options {
  3400     self.renderer_state.mutex.lockUncancelable(global.io());
  3401     defer self.renderer_state.mutex.unlock(global.io());
  3402     const t = &self.io.terminal;
  3403 
  3404     var opts: input.key_encode.Options = .fromTerminal(t);
  3405     if (comptime builtin.target.os.tag != .macos) return opts;
  3406 
  3407     opts.macos_option_as_alt = self.config.macos_option_as_alt orelse detect: {
  3408         // If we don't have alt pressed, it doesn't matter what this
  3409         // config is so we can just say "false" and break out and avoid
  3410         // more expensive checks below.
  3411         if (!self.mouse.mods.alt) break :detect .false;
  3412 
  3413         // Alt is pressed, we're on macOS. We break some encapsulation
  3414         // here and assume libghostty for ease...
  3415         break :detect self.rt_app.keyboardLayout().detectOptionAsAlt();
  3416     };
  3417 
  3418     return opts;
  3419 }

  4941 pub fn performBindingAction(self: *Surface, action: input.Binding.Action) !bool {
  4942     // Forward app-scoped actions to the app. Some app-scoped actions are
  4943     // special-cased here because they do some special things when performed
  4944     // from the surface.
  4945     if (action.scoped(.app)) |app_action| {
  4946         switch (app_action) {
  4947             .new_window => try self.app.newWindow(
  4948                 self.rt_app,
  4949                 .{ .parent = self },
  4950             ),
  4951 
  4952             // Undo and redo both support both surface and app targeting.
  4953             // If we are triggering on a surface then we perform the
  4954             // action with the surface target.
  4955             .undo => return try self.rt_app.performAction(
  4956                 .{ .surface = self },
  4957                 .undo,
  4958                 {},
  4959             ),
  4960 
  4961             .redo => return try self.rt_app.performAction(
  4962                 .{ .surface = self },
  4963                 .redo,
  4964                 {},
  4965             ),
  4966 
  4967             else => try self.app.performAction(
  4968                 self.rt_app,
  4969                 action.scoped(.app).?,
  4970             ),
  4971         }
  4972         return true;
  4973     }
  4974 
  4975     switch (action.scoped(.surface).?) {

n25 src/Surface.zig:5821–5833 DI
  5821 /// Returns true if performing the given action result in closing
  5822 /// the surface. This is used to determine if our self pointer is
  5823 /// still valid after performing some binding action.
  5824 fn closingAction(action: input.Binding.Action) bool {
  5825     return switch (action) {
  5826         .close_surface,
  5827         .close_window,
  5828         .close_tab,
  5829         => true,
  5830 
  5831         else => false,
  5832     };
  5833 }

n25 src/apprt/embedded.zig:104–137 DIU
   104     pub const KeyEvent = struct {
   105         action: input.Action,
   106         mods: input.Mods,
   107         consumed_mods: input.Mods,
   108         keycode: u32,
   109         text: ?[:0]const u8,
   110         unshifted_codepoint: u32,
   111         composing: bool,
   112 
   113         /// Convert a libghostty key event into a core key event.
   114         fn core(self: KeyEvent) ?input.KeyEvent {
   115             const text: []const u8 = if (self.text) |v| v else "";
   116             const unshifted_codepoint: u21 = std.math.cast(
   117                 u21,
   118                 self.unshifted_codepoint,
   119             ) orelse 0;
   120 
   121             // We want to get the physical unmapped key to process keybinds.
   122             const physical_key = keycode: for (input.keycodes.entries) |entry| {
   123                 if (entry.native == self.keycode) break :keycode entry.key;
   124             } else .unidentified;
   125 
   126             // Build our final key event
   127             return .{
   128                 .action = self.action,
   129                 .key = physical_key,
   130                 .mods = self.mods,
   131                 .consumed_mods = self.consumed_mods,
   132                 .composing = self.composing,
   133                 .utf8 = text,
   134                 .unshifted_codepoint = unshifted_codepoint,
   135             };
   136         }
   137     };

n25 src/apprt/embedded.zig:201–228 DIU
   201     /// See CoreApp.keyEvent.
   202     pub fn keyEvent(
   203         self: *App,
   204         target: KeyTarget,
   205         event: KeyEvent,
   206     ) !bool {
   207         // Convert our C key event into a Zig one.
   208         const input_event: input.KeyEvent = event.core() orelse
   209             return false;
   210 
   211         // Invoke the core Ghostty logic to handle this input.
   212         const effect: CoreSurface.InputEffect = switch (target) {
   213             .app => if (self.core_app.keyEvent(
   214                 self,
   215                 input_event,
   216             )) .consumed else .ignored,
   217 
   218             .surface => |surface| try surface.core_surface.keyCallback(
   219                 input_event,
   220             ),
   221         };
   222 
   223         return switch (effect) {
   224             .closed => true,
   225             .ignored => false,
   226             .consumed => true,
   227         };
   228     }

n25 src/apprt/embedded.zig:2070–2084 DIU
  2070     /// Send this for raw keypresses (i.e. the keyDown event on macOS).
  2071     /// This will handle the keymap translation and send the appropriate
  2072     /// key and char events.
  2073     export fn ghostty_surface_key(
  2074         surface: *Surface,
  2075         event: KeyEvent,
  2076     ) bool {
  2077         return surface.app.keyEvent(
  2078             .{ .surface = surface },
  2079             event.keyEvent(),
  2080         ) catch |err| {
  2081             log.warn("error processing key event err={}", .{err});
  2082             return false;
  2083         };
  2084     }

n25 src/apprt/gtk/class/surface.zig:1275–1282 D

  1276         self: *Surface,
  1277         action: input.Action,

n25 src/apprt/gtk/class/surface.zig:1378–1417 IU
  1378         // Get the keyvals for this event.
  1379         const keyval_unicode = gdk.keyvalToUnicode(keyval);
  1380         const keyval_unicode_unshifted: u21 = gtk_key.keyvalUnicodeUnshifted(
  1381             priv.render_surface.as(gtk.Widget),
  1382             key_event,
  1383             keycode,
  1384         );
  1385 
  1386         // We want to get the physical unmapped key to process physical keybinds.
  1387         // (These are keybinds explicitly marked as requesting physical mapping).
  1388         const physical_key = keycode: {
  1389             const w3c_key: input.Key = w3c: for (input.keycodes.entries) |entry| {
  1390                 if (entry.native == keycode) break :w3c entry.key;
  1391             } else .unidentified;
  1392 
  1393             break :keycode gtk_key.remapKey(
  1394                 w3c_key,
  1395                 keyval,
  1396                 key_event.isModifier() != 0,
  1397             );
  1398         };
  1399 
  1400         // Get our modifier for the event
  1401         const mods: input.Mods = gtk_key.eventMods(
  1402             event,
  1403             physical_key,
  1404             gtk_mods,
  1405             action,
  1406             Application.default().winproto(),
  1407         );
  1408 
  1409         // Get our consumed modifiers
  1410         const consumed_mods: input.Mods = consumed: {
  1411             const T = @typeInfo(gdk.ModifierType);
  1412             std.debug.assert(T.@"struct".layout == .@"packed");
  1413             const I = T.@"struct".backing_integer.?;
  1414 
  1415             const masked = @as(I, @bitCast(key_event.getConsumedModifiers())) & @as(I, gdk.MODIFIER_MASK);
  1416             break :consumed gtk_key.translateMods(@bitCast(masked));
  1417         };

n25 src/apprt/gtk/class/surface.zig:1445–1482 IU
  1445         // Invoke the core Ghostty logic to handle this input.
  1446         const surface = priv.core_surface orelse return false;
  1447         const effect = surface.keyCallback(.{
  1448             .action = action,
  1449             .key = physical_key,
  1450             .mods = mods,
  1451             .consumed_mods = consumed_mods,
  1452             .composing = priv.im_composing,
  1453             .utf8 = priv.im_buf[0..priv.im_len],
  1454             .unshifted_codepoint = keyval_unicode_unshifted,
  1455         }) catch |err| {
  1456             log.err("error in key callback err={}", .{err});
  1457             return false;
  1458         };
  1459 
  1460         switch (effect) {
  1461             .closed => return true,
  1462             .ignored => {},
  1463             .consumed => if (action == .press or action == .repeat) {
  1464                 // If we were in the composing state then we reset our context.
  1465                 // We do NOT want to reset if we're not in the composing state
  1466                 // because there is other IME state that we want to preserve,
  1467                 // such as quotation mark ordering for Chinese input.
  1468                 if (priv.im_composing) {
  1469                     priv.im_context.as(gtk.IMContext).reset();
  1470                     surface.preeditCallback(null) catch {};
  1471                 }
  1472 
  1473                 // Bell stops ringing when any key is pressed that is used by
  1474                 // the core in any way.
  1475                 self.setBellRinging(false);
  1476 
  1477                 return true;
  1478             },
  1479         }
  1480 
  1481         return false;
  1482     }

n25 src/input/Binding.zig:2705–2708 DI
  2705     /// Get a binding for a given trigger.
  2706     pub fn get(self: Set, t: Trigger) ?Entry {
  2707         return self.bindings.getEntry(t);
  2708     }

n25 src/input/Binding.zig:2716–2760 DIU
  2716     /// Get an entry for the given key event. This will attempt to find
  2717     /// a binding using multiple parts of the event in the following order:
  2718     ///
  2719     ///   1. Physical key (event.physical_key)
  2720     ///   2. Unshifted Unicode codepoint (event.unshifted_codepoint)
  2721     ///
  2722     pub fn getEvent(self: *const Set, event: KeyEvent) ?Entry {
  2723         var trigger: Trigger = .{
  2724             .mods = event.mods.binding(),
  2725             .key = .{ .physical = event.key },
  2726         };
  2727         if (self.get(trigger)) |v| return v;
  2728 
  2729         // If our UTF-8 text is exactly one codepoint, we try to match that.
  2730         if (event.utf8.len > 0) unicode: {
  2731             const view = std.unicode.Utf8View.init(event.utf8) catch break :unicode;
  2732             var it = view.iterator();
  2733 
  2734             // No codepoints or multiple codepoints drops to invalid format
  2735             const cp = it.nextCodepoint() orelse break :unicode;
  2736             if (it.nextCodepoint() != null) break :unicode;
  2737 
  2738             trigger.key = .{ .unicode = cp };
  2739             if (self.get(trigger)) |v| return v;
  2740         }
  2741 
  2742         // Finally fallback to the full unshifted codepoint if we have one.
  2743         // Question: should we be doing this if we have UTF-8 text? I
  2744         // suspect "no" but we don't currently have any failing scenarios
  2745         // to verify this.
  2746         if (event.unshifted_codepoint > 0) {
  2747             trigger.key = .{ .unicode = event.unshifted_codepoint };
  2748             if (self.get(trigger)) |v| return v;
  2749         }
  2750 
  2751         // Fallback to catch_all with modifiers first, then without modifiers.
  2752         trigger.key = .catch_all;
  2753         if (self.get(trigger)) |v| return v;
  2754         if (!trigger.mods.empty()) {
  2755             trigger.mods = .{};
  2756             if (self.get(trigger)) |v| return v;
  2757         }
  2758 
  2759         return null;
  2760     }

n25 src/input/function_keys.zig:12–49 D
    12 pub const CursorMode = enum { any, normal, application };
    13 pub const KeypadMode = enum { any, normal, application };
    14 
    15 /// A bit confusing so I'll document this one: this is the "modify other keys"
    16 /// setting. We only change behavior for "set_other" which is ESC [ > 4; 2 m.
    17 /// So this can be "any" which means we don't care what's going on. Or it
    18 /// can be "set" which means modify keys must be set EXCEPT FOR "other keys"
    19 /// mode, and "set_other" which means modify keys must be set to "other keys"
    20 /// mode.
    21 ///
    22 /// See: https://invisible-island.net/xterm/modified-keys.html
    23 pub const ModifyKeys = enum {
    24     any,
    25     set,
    26     set_other,
    27 };
    28 
    29 /// A single entry in the table of keys.
    30 pub const Entry = struct {
    31     /// The exact set of modifiers that must be active for this entry to match.
    32     /// If mods_empty_is_any is true then empty mods means any set of mods can
    33     /// match. Otherwise, empty mods means no mods must be active.
    34     mods: key.Mods = .{},
    35     mods_empty_is_any: bool = true,
    36 
    37     /// The state required for cursor/keypad mode.
    38     cursor: CursorMode = .any,
    39     keypad: KeypadMode = .any,
    40 
    41     /// Whether or not this entry should be used
    42     modify_other_keys: ModifyKeys = .any,
    43 
    44     /// The sequence to send to the pty if this entry matches.
    45     sequence: []const u8,
    46 
    47     /// Sequence to send to the PTY if DECBKM is set.
    48     sequence_decbkm: ?[]const u8 = null,
    49 };

   196         // Everything else
   197         .{ .mods = .{ .alt = true, .shift = true }, .sequence = "\x1b[27;4;9~" },
   198         .{ .mods = .{ .ctrl = true }, .sequence = "\x1b[27;5;9~" },

   201         .{ .mods = .{ .alt = true, .ctrl = true, .shift = true }, .sequence = "\x1b[27;8;9~" },
   202         .{ .mods = .{ .super = true }, .sequence = "\x1b[27;9;9~" },
   203         .{ .mods = .{ .super = true, .shift = true }, .sequence = "\x1b[27;10;9~" },
   204         .{ .mods = .{ .alt = true, .super = true }, .sequence = "\x1b[27;11;9~" },
   205         .{ .mods = .{ .alt = true, .super = true, .shift = true }, .sequence = "\x1b[27;12;9~" },
   206         .{ .mods = .{ .super = true, .ctrl = true }, .sequence = "\x1b[27;13;9~" },
   207         .{ .mods = .{ .super = true, .ctrl = true, .shift = true }, .sequence = "\x1b[27;14;9~" },
   208         .{ .mods = .{ .alt = true, .super = true, .ctrl = true }, .sequence = "\x1b[27;15;9~" },
   209         .{ .mods = .{ .alt = true, .super = true, .ctrl = true, .shift = true }, .sequence = "\x1b[27;16;9~" },
   210

   212     });
   213 
   214     result.set(.enter, &.{
   215         .{ .mods = .{ .shift = true }, .sequence = "\x1b[27;2;13~" },
   216 
   217         // Modify Keys Normal
   218         .{ .mods = .{ .alt = true }, .modify_other_keys = .set, .sequence = "\x1b\r" },
   219 
   220         // Modify Keys Other
   221         .{ .mods = .{ .alt = true }, .modify_other_keys = .set_other, .sequence = "\x1b[27;3;13~" },
   222 
   223         // Everything else
   224         .{ .mods = .{ .alt = true, .shift = true }, .sequence = "\x1b[27;4;13~" },
   225         .{ .mods = .{ .ctrl = true }, .sequence = "\x1b[27;5;13~" },
   226         .{ .mods = .{ .ctrl = true, .shift = true }, .sequence = "\x1b[27;6;13~" },
   227         .{ .mods = .{ .alt = true, .ctrl = true }, .sequence = "\x1b[27;7;13~" },
   228         .{ .mods = .{ .alt = true, .ctrl = true, .shift = true }, .sequence = "\x1b[27;8;13~" },
   229         .{ .mods = .{ .super = true }, .sequence = "\x1b[27;9;13~" },
   230         .{ .mods = .{ .super = true, .shift = true }, .sequence = "\x1b[27;10;13~" },
   231         .{ .mods = .{ .alt = true, .super = true }, .sequence = "\x1b[27;11;13~" },
   232         .{ .mods = .{ .alt = true, .super = true, .shift = true }, .sequence = "\x1b[27;12;13~" },
   233         .{ .mods = .{ .super = true, .ctrl = true }, .sequence = "\x1b[27;13;13~" },
   234         .{ .mods = .{ .super = true, .ctrl = true, .shift = true }, .sequence = "\x1b[27;14;13~" },
   235         .{ .mods = .{ .alt = true, .super = true, .ctrl = true }, .sequence = "\x1b[27;15;13~" },
   236         .{ .mods = .{ .alt = true, .super = true, .ctrl = true, .shift = true }, .sequence = "\x1b[27;16;13~" },

   241     result.set(.escape, &.{
   242         .{ .mods = .{ .shift = true }, .sequence = "\x1b[27;2;27~" },
   243         .{ .mods = .{ .alt = true }, .modify_other_keys = .set, .sequence = "\x1b\x1b" },
   244         .{ .mods = .{ .alt = true }, .modify_other_keys = .set_other, .sequence = "\x1b[27;3;27~" },
   245         .{ .mods = .{ .alt = true, .shift = true }, .sequence = "\x1b[27;4;27~" },
   246         .{ .mods = .{ .ctrl = true }, .sequence = "\x1b[27;5;27~" },
   247         .{ .mods = .{ .ctrl = true, .shift = true }, .sequence = "\x1b[27;6;27~" },
   248         .{ .mods = .{ .alt = true, .ctrl = true }, .sequence = "\x1b[27;7;27~" },
   249         .{ .mods = .{ .alt = true, .ctrl = true, .shift = true }, .sequence = "\x1b[27;8;27~" },
   250         .{ .mods = .{ .super = true }, .sequence = "\x1b[27;9;27~" },
   251         .{ .mods = .{ .super = true, .shift = true }, .sequence = "\x1b[27;10;27~" },
   252         .{ .mods = .{ .alt = true, .super = true }, .sequence = "\x1b[27;11;27~" },
   253         .{ .mods = .{ .alt = true, .super = true, .shift = true }, .sequence = "\x1b[27;12;27~" },
   254         .{ .mods = .{ .super = true, .ctrl = true }, .sequence = "\x1b[27;13;27~" },
   255         .{ .mods = .{ .super = true, .ctrl = true, .shift = true }, .sequence = "\x1b[27;14;27~" },
   256         .{ .mods = .{ .alt = true, .super = true, .ctrl = true }, .sequence = "\x1b[27;15;27~" },
   257         .{ .mods = .{ .alt = true, .super = true, .ctrl = true, .shift = true }, .sequence = "\x1b[27;16;27~" },
   258         .{ .sequence = "\x1b" },
   259     });
   260 
   261     break :keys result;
   262 };

n25 src/input/function_keys.zig:264–314 DIU
   264 /// Returns the default keypad application mode entry.
   265 fn kpDefault(comptime suffix: []const u8) []const Entry {
   266     return &.{
   267         .{
   268             .mods_empty_is_any = false,
   269             .keypad = .application,
   270             .sequence = "\x1bO" ++ suffix,
   271         },
   272     };
   273 }
   274 
   275 /// Returns the entries for a keypad key. The suffix is the final character
   276 /// of the sent sequence, such as "r" for kp_2.
   277 fn kpKeys(comptime suffix: []const u8, comptime normal: []const u8) []const Entry {
   278     const pc = pcStyle("\x1bO{}" ++ suffix);
   279     for (pc) |*entry| entry.keypad = .application;
   280     return kpDefault(suffix) ++ pc ++ .{
   281         Entry{ .keypad = .normal, .sequence = normal },
   282     };
   283 }
   284 
   285 /// Returns entries that are dependent on cursor key settings.
   286 fn cursorKey(
   287     comptime normal: []const u8,
   288     comptime application: []const u8,
   289 ) []const Entry {
   290     return &.{
   291         .{ .cursor = .normal, .sequence = normal },
   292         .{ .cursor = .application, .sequence = application },
   293     };
   294 }
   295 
   296 /// Constructs a set of pcStyle function keys using the given format. The
   297 /// format should have exactly one "hole" for the mods code.
   298 /// Example: "\x1b[11;{}~" for F1.
   299 fn pcStyle(comptime fmt: []const u8) []Entry {
   300     // The comptime {} wrapper is superfluous but it prevents us from
   301     // accidentally running this function at runtime.
   302     comptime {
   303         @setEvalBranchQuota(500_000);
   304         var entries: [modifiers.len]Entry = undefined;
   305         for (modifiers, 2.., 0..) |mods, code, i| {
   306             entries[i] = .{
   307                 .mods = mods,
   308                 .sequence = std.fmt.comptimePrint(fmt, .{code}),
   309             };
   310         }
   311 
   312         return &entries;
   313     }
   314 }

n25 src/input/key.zig:16–55 DI
    16 pub const KeyEvent = struct {
    17     /// The action: press, release, etc.
    18     action: Action = .press,
    19 
    20     /// The keycode of the physical key that was pressed. This is agnostic
    21     /// to the layout. Layout-dependent matching can only be done via the
    22     /// UTF-8 or unshifted codepoint.
    23     key: Key = .unidentified,
    24 
    25     /// Mods are the modifiers that are pressed.
    26     mods: Mods = .{},
    27 
    28     /// The mods that were consumed in order to generate the text
    29     /// in utf8. This has the mods set that were consumed, so to
    30     /// get the set of mods that are effective you must negate
    31     /// mods with this.
    32     ///
    33     /// This field is meaningless if utf8 is empty.
    34     consumed_mods: Mods = .{},
    35 
    36     /// Composing is true when this key event is part of a dead key
    37     /// composition sequence and we're in the middle of it.
    38     composing: bool = false,
    39 
    40     /// The utf8 sequence that was generated by this key event.
    41     /// This will be an empty string if there is no text generated.
    42     /// If composing is true and this is non-empty, this is preedit
    43     /// text.
    44     utf8: []const u8 = "",
    45 
    46     /// The codepoint for this key when it is unshifted. For example,
    47     /// shift+a is "A" in UTF-8 but unshifted would provide 'a'.
    48     unshifted_codepoint: u21 = 0,
    49 
    50     /// Returns the effective modifiers for this event. The effective
    51     /// modifiers are the mods that should be considered for keybindings.
    52     pub fn effectiveMods(self: KeyEvent) Mods {
    53         if (self.utf8.len == 0) return self.mods;
    54         return self.mods.unset(self.consumed_mods);
    55     }

n25 src/input/key.zig:57–78 DI
    57     /// Returns a unique hash for this key event to be used for tracking
    58     /// uniquess specifically with bindings. This omits fields that are
    59     /// irrelevant for bindings.
    60     pub fn bindingHash(self: KeyEvent) u64 {
    61         var hasher = std.hash.Wyhash.init(0);
    62 
    63         // These are all the fields that are explicitly part of Trigger.
    64         std.hash.autoHash(&hasher, self.key);
    65         std.hash.autoHash(&hasher, self.unshifted_codepoint);
    66         std.hash.autoHash(&hasher, self.mods.binding());
    67 
    68         // Notes on unmapped things and why:
    69         //
    70         // - action: we don't have action-specific bindings right now
    71         //   AND we want to know if a key resulted in a binding regardless
    72         //   of action because a press should also ignore a release and so on.
    73         //
    74         // We can add to this if there is other confusion.
    75 
    76         return hasher.final();
    77     }
    78 };

n25 src/input/key_encode.zig:55–97 DIU
    55     /// Initialize our options from the terminal state.
    56     ///
    57     /// Note that `macos_option_as_alt` cannot be determined from
    58     /// terminal state so it must be set manually after this call.
    59     pub fn fromTerminal(t: *const Terminal) Options {
    60         return .{
    61             .alt_esc_prefix = t.modes.get(.alt_esc_prefix),
    62             .cursor_key_application = t.modes.get(.cursor_keys),
    63             .keypad_key_application = t.modes.get(.keypad_keys),
    64             .backarrow_key_mode = t.modes.get(.backarrow_key_mode),
    65             .ignore_keypad_with_numlock = t.modes.get(.ignore_keypad_with_numlock),
    66             .modify_other_keys_state_2 = t.flags.modify_other_keys_2,
    67             .kitty_flags = t.screens.active.kitty_keyboard.current(),
    68 
    69             // These can't be known from the terminal state.
    70             .macos_option_as_alt = .false,
    71         };
    72     }
    73 };
    74 
    75 /// Encode the key event to the writer in the proper format given
    76 /// the options. For example, this will properly encode a key press
    77 /// such as "ctrl+A" to Kitty format if Kitty encoding is enabled.
    78 ///
    79 /// Not all key events will result in output. It is up to the caller
    80 /// to use a writer that can track whether any output was written if
    81 /// they care about that.
    82 pub fn encode(
    83     writer: *std.Io.Writer,
    84     event: key.KeyEvent,
    85     opts: Options,
    86 ) std.Io.Writer.Error!void {
    87     //std.log.warn("KEYENCODER event={} opts={}", .{ event, opts });
    88     return if (opts.kitty_flags.int() != 0) try kitty(
    89         writer,
    90         event,
    91         opts,
    92     ) else try legacy(
    93         writer,
    94         event,
    95         opts,
    96     );
    97 }

n25 src/input/key_encode.zig:99–319 DIU
    99 /// Perform Kitty keyboard protocol encoding of the key event.
   100 fn kitty(
   101     writer: *std.Io.Writer,
   102     event: key.KeyEvent,
   103     opts: Options,
   104 ) std.Io.Writer.Error!void {
   105     // This should never happen but we'll check anyway.
   106     if (opts.kitty_flags.int() == 0) return try legacy(
   107         writer,
   108         event,
   109         opts,
   110     );
   111 
   112     // We only processed "press" events unless report events is active
   113     if (event.action == .release) {
   114         if (!opts.kitty_flags.report_events) return;
   115 
   116         // Enter, backspace, and tab do not report release events unless "report
   117         // all" is set
   118         if (!opts.kitty_flags.report_all) {
   119             switch (event.key) {
   120                 .enter, .backspace, .tab => return,
   121                 else => {},
   122             }
   123         }
   124     }
   125 
   126     const all_mods = event.mods;
   127     const effective_mods = event.effectiveMods();
   128     const binding_mods = effective_mods.binding();
   129 
   130     // Find the entry for this key in the kitty table.
   131     const entry_: ?KittyEntry = entry: {
   132         // Functional or predefined keys
   133         for (kitty_entries) |entry| {
   134             if (entry.key == event.key) break :entry entry;
   135         }
   136 
   137         // Otherwise, we use our unicode codepoint from UTF8. We
   138         // always use the unshifted value.
   139         if (event.unshifted_codepoint > 0) {
   140             break :entry .{
   141                 .key = event.key,
   142                 .code = event.unshifted_codepoint,
   143                 .final = 'u',
   144                 .modifier = false,
   145             };
   146         }
   147 
   148         break :entry null;
   149     };
   150 
   151     preprocessing: {
   152         // When composing, the only keys sent are plain modifiers.
   153         if (event.composing) {
   154             if (entry_) |entry| {
   155                 if (entry.modifier) break :preprocessing;
   156             }
   157 
   158             return;
   159         }
   160 
   161         // IME confirmation still sends an enter key so if we have enter
   162         // and UTF8 text we just send it directly since we assume that is
   163         // what's happening. See legacy()'s similar logic for more details
   164         // on how to verify this.
   165         if (event.utf8.len > 0) utf8: {
   166             switch (event.key) {
   167                 else => {},
   168                 inline .enter, .backspace => |tag| {
   169                     // See legacy for why we handle this way.
   170                     if (isControlUtf8(event.utf8)) break :utf8;
   171                     if (comptime tag == .backspace) return;
   172                     return try writer.writeAll(event.utf8);
   173                 },
   174             }
   175         }
   176 
   177         // If we're reporting all then we always send CSI sequences.
   178         if (!opts.kitty_flags.report_all) {
   179             // Quote:
   180             // The only exceptions are the Enter, Tab and Backspace keys which
   181             // still generate the same bytes as in legacy mode this is to allow the
   182             // user to type and execute commands in the shell such as reset after a
   183             // program that sets this mode crashes without clearing it.
   184             //
   185             // Quote ("report all" mode):
   186             // Note that all keys are reported as escape codes, including Enter,
   187             // Tab, Backspace etc.
   188             if (binding_mods.empty()) {
   189                 switch (event.key) {
   190                     .enter => return try writer.writeByte('\r'),
   191                     .tab => return try writer.writeByte('\t'),
   192                     .backspace => return try writer.writeByte(0x7F),
   193                     else => {},
   194                 }
   195             }
   196 
   197             // Send plain-text non-modified text directly to the terminal.
   198             // We don't send release events because those are specially encoded.
   199             if (event.utf8.len > 0 and
   200                 binding_mods.empty() and
   201                 event.action != .release)
   202             plain_text: {
   203                 // We only do this for printable characters. We should
   204                 // inspect the real unicode codepoint properties here but
   205                 // the real world issue is usually control characters.
   206                 const view = std.unicode.Utf8View.init(event.utf8) catch {
   207                     // Invalid UTF-8 so let's fallback to encoding the
   208                     // key press as if it didn't produce UTF-8 text. I'm
   209                     // not sure what should happen here according to the spec,
   210                     // since it doesn't specify this behavior. Presumably
   211                     // this is a caller bug.
   212                     break :plain_text;
   213                 };
   214                 var it = view.iterator();
   215                 while (it.nextCodepoint()) |cp| {
   216                     if (isControl(cp)) break :plain_text;
   217                 }
   218 
   219                 return try writer.writeAll(event.utf8);
   220             }
   221         }
   222     }
   223 
   224     const entry = entry_ orelse {
   225         // No entry found. If we have UTF-8 text this is a pure text event
   226         // (e.g. composed/IME text), so send it as-is so programs can
   227         // still receive it. Release events never insert text, same as the
   228         // plain-text path above.
   229         if (event.action == .release) return;
   230         if (event.utf8.len == 0) return;
   231         return try writer.writeAll(event.utf8);
   232     };
   233 
   234     // If this is just a modifier we require "report all" to send the sequence.
   235     if (entry.modifier and !opts.kitty_flags.report_all) return;
   236 
   237     const seq: KittySequence = seq: {
   238         var seq: KittySequence = .{
   239             .key = entry.code,
   240             .final = entry.final,
   241             .mods = .fromInput(
   242                 event.action,
   243                 event.key,
   244                 all_mods,
   245             ),
   246         };
   247 
   248         if (opts.kitty_flags.report_events) {
   249             seq.event = switch (event.action) {
   250                 .press => .press,
   251                 .release => .release,
   252                 .repeat => .repeat,
   253             };
   254         }
   255 
   256         if (opts.kitty_flags.report_alternates) alternates: {
   257             // Break early if this is a control key
   258             if (isControl(seq.key)) break :alternates;
   259 
   260             const view = std.unicode.Utf8View.init(event.utf8) catch {
   261                 // Assume invalid UTF-8 means no UTF-8.
   262                 break :alternates;
   263             };
   264             var it = view.iterator();
   265 
   266             // If we have a codepoint in our UTF-8 sequence, then we can
   267             // report the shifted version.
   268             if (it.nextCodepoint()) |cp1| {
   269                 // Set the first alternate (shifted version)
   270                 if (cp1 != seq.key and seq.mods.shift) seq.alternates[0] = cp1;
   271 
   272                 // We want to know if there are additional codepoints because
   273                 // our logic below depends on the utf8 being a single codepoint.
   274                 const has_cp2 = it.nextCodepoint() != null;
   275 
   276                 // Set the base layout key. We only report this if this codepoint
   277                 // differs from our pressed key.
   278                 if (event.key.codepoint()) |base| {
   279                     if (base != seq.key and
   280                         (cp1 != base and !has_cp2))
   281                     {
   282                         seq.alternates[1] = base;
   283                     }
   284                 }
   285             } else {
   286                 // No UTF-8 so we can't report a shifted key but we can still
   287                 // report a base layout key.
   288                 if (event.key.codepoint()) |base| {
   289                     if (base != seq.key) seq.alternates[1] = base;
   290                 }
   291             }
   292         }
   293 
   294         if (opts.kitty_flags.report_associated and
   295             seq.event != .release)
   296         associated: {
   297             // Determine if the Alt modifier should be treated as an actual
   298             // modifier (in which case it prevents associated text) or as
   299             // the macOS Option key, which does not prevent associated text.
   300             const alt_prevents_text = if (comptime builtin.target.os.tag == .macos)
   301                 switch (opts.macos_option_as_alt) {
   302                     .left => all_mods.sides.alt == .left,
   303                     .right => all_mods.sides.alt == .right,
   304                     .true => true,
   305                     .false => false,
   306                 }
   307             else
   308                 true;
   309 
   310             if (seq.mods.preventsText(alt_prevents_text)) break :associated;
   311 
   312             seq.text = event.utf8;
   313         }
   314 
   315         break :seq seq;
   316     };
   317 
   318     return try seq.encode(writer);
   319 }

n25 src/input/key_encode.zig:327–351 DIU
   327 fn legacy(
   328     writer: *std.Io.Writer,
   329     event: key.KeyEvent,
   330     opts: Options,
   331 ) std.Io.Writer.Error!void {
   332     const all_mods = event.mods;
   333     const effective_mods = event.effectiveMods();
   334     const binding_mods = effective_mods.binding();
   335 
   336     // Legacy encoding only does press/repeat
   337     if (event.action != .press and event.action != .repeat) return;
   338 
   339     // If we're in a dead key state then we never emit a sequence.
   340     if (event.composing) return;
   341 
   342     // If we match a PC style function key then that is our result.
   343     if (pcStyleFunctionKey(
   344         event.key,
   345         all_mods,
   346         opts.cursor_key_application,
   347         opts.keypad_key_application,
   348         opts.ignore_keypad_with_numlock,
   349         opts.modify_other_keys_state_2,
   350         opts.backarrow_key_mode,
   351     )) |sequence| pc_style: {

n25 src/input/key_encode.zig:352–378 I
   352         // If we have UTF-8 text, then we never emit PC style function
   353         // keys. Many function keys (escape, enter, backspace) have
   354         // a specific meaning when dead keys are active and so we don't
   355         // want to send that to the terminal. Examples:
   356         //
   357         //   - Japanese: escape clears the dead key state
   358         //   - Korean: escape commits the dead key state
   359         //   - Korean: backspace should delete a single preedit char
   360         //
   361         if (event.utf8.len > 0) utf8: {
   362             switch (event.key) {
   363                 else => {},
   364                 inline .backspace, .enter, .escape => |tag| {
   365                     // We want to ignore control characters. This is because
   366                     // some apprts (macOS) will send control characters as
   367                     // UTF-8 encodings and we handle that manually.
   368                     if (isControlUtf8(event.utf8)) break :utf8;
   369 
   370                     // Backspace encodes nothing because we modified IME.
   371                     // Enter/escape don't encode the PC-style encoding
   372                     // because we want to encode committed text.
   373                     if (comptime tag == .backspace) return;
   374                     break :pc_style;
   375                 },
   376             }
   377         }
   378 

n25 src/input/key_encode.zig:379–550 IU
   379         return try writer.writeAll(sequence);
   380     }
   381 
   382     // In modify other keys state 2, we send the CSI 27 sequence
   383     // for any char with a modifier. This must happen before converting
   384     // Ctrl+<char> to a C0 byte because mode 2 encodes those keys, too.
   385     const utf8 = event.utf8;
   386     if (opts.modify_other_keys_state_2) modify_other: {
   387         const view = std.unicode.Utf8View.init(utf8) catch {
   388             // Assume invalid UTF-8 means we no UTF-8.
   389             break :modify_other;
   390         };
   391         var it = view.iterator();
   392         const codepoint = it.nextCodepoint() orelse break :modify_other;
   393 
   394         // We only do this if we have a single codepoint. There shouldn't
   395         // ever be a multi-codepoint sequence that triggers this.
   396         if (it.nextCodepoint() != null) break :modify_other;
   397 
   398         // The mods we encode for this are just the binding mods (shift, ctrl,
   399         // super, alt unless it is actually option).
   400         const mods = mods: {
   401             var mods_binding = event.mods.binding();
   402             if (comptime builtin.target.os.tag.isDarwin()) alt: {
   403                 switch (opts.macos_option_as_alt) {
   404                     .false => {},
   405                     .true => break :alt,
   406                     .left => if (event.mods.sides.alt == .left) break :alt,
   407                     .right => if (event.mods.sides.alt == .right) break :alt,
   408                 }
   409                 mods_binding.alt = false;
   410             }
   411             break :mods mods_binding;
   412         };
   413 
   414         // This copies xterm's `ModifyOtherKeys` function that returns
   415         // whether modify other keys should be encoded for the given
   416         // input.
   417         const should_modify = should_modify: {
   418             // xterm IsControlInput
   419             if (codepoint >= 0x40 and codepoint <= 0x7F)
   420                 break :should_modify true;
   421 
   422             // If we have anything other than shift pressed, encode.
   423             var mods_no_shift = mods;
   424             mods_no_shift.shift = false;
   425             if (!mods_no_shift.empty()) break :should_modify true;
   426 
   427             // We only have shift pressed. We only allow space.
   428             if (codepoint == ' ') break :should_modify true;
   429 
   430             // This logic isn't complete but I don't fully understand
   431             // the rest so I'm going to wait until we can have a
   432             // reasonable test scenario.
   433             break :should_modify false;
   434         };
   435 
   436         if (should_modify) {
   437             for (function_keys.modifiers, 2..) |modset, code| {
   438                 if (!mods.equal(modset)) continue;
   439                 return try writer.print(
   440                     "\x1B[27;{};{}~",
   441                     .{ code, codepoint },
   442                 );
   443             }
   444         }
   445     }
   446 
   447     // If we match a control sequence, we output that directly. For
   448     // ctrlSeq we have to use all mods because we want it to only
   449     // match ctrl+<char>.
   450     if (ctrlSeq(
   451         event.key,
   452         event.utf8,
   453         event.unshifted_codepoint,
   454         all_mods,
   455     )) |char| {
   456         // C0 sequences support alt-as-esc prefixing.
   457         if (binding_mods.alt) {
   458             try writer.writeByte(0x1B);
   459             try writer.writeByte(char);
   460             return;
   461         }
   462 
   463         try writer.writeByte(char);
   464         return;
   465     }
   466 
   467     // If we have no UTF8 text then the only possibility is the
   468     // alt-prefix handling of unshifted codepoints... so we process that.
   469     if (utf8.len == 0) {
   470         _ = try legacyAltPrefix(
   471             writer,
   472             event,
   473             binding_mods,
   474             all_mods,
   475             opts,
   476         );
   477         return;
   478     }
   479 
   480     // Let's see if we should apply fixterms to this codepoint.
   481     // At this stage of key processing, we only need to apply fixterms
   482     // to unicode codepoints if we have ctrl set.
   483     if (event.mods.ctrl) csiu: {
   484         // Important: we want to use the original mods here, not the
   485         // effective mods. The fixterms spec states the shifted chars
   486         // should be sent uppercase but Kitty changes that behavior
   487         // so we'll send all the mods.
   488         const csi_u_mods, const char = mods: {
   489             var mods = CsiUMods.fromInput(event.mods);
   490 
   491             // Get our codepoint. If we have more than one codepoint this
   492             // can't be valid CSIu.
   493             const view = std.unicode.Utf8View.init(event.utf8) catch break :csiu;
   494             var it = view.iterator();
   495             var char = it.nextCodepoint() orelse break :csiu;
   496             if (it.nextCodepoint() != null) break :csiu;
   497 
   498             // If our character is A to Z and we have shift set, then
   499             // we lowercase it. This is a Kitty-specific behavior that
   500             // we choose to follow and diverge from the fixterms spec.
   501             // This makes it easier for programs to detect shifted letters
   502             // for keybindings and is not just theoretical but used by
   503             // real programs.
   504             if (char >= 'A' and char <= 'Z' and mods.shift) {
   505                 // We want to rely on apprt to send us the correct
   506                 // unshifted codepoint...
   507                 char = @intCast(std.ascii.toLower(@intCast(char)));
   508             }
   509 
   510             // If our unshifted codepoint is identical to the shifted
   511             // then we consider shift. Otherwise, we do not because the
   512             // shift key was used to obtain the character. This is specified
   513             // by fixterms.
   514             if (event.unshifted_codepoint != char) {
   515                 mods.shift = false;
   516             }
   517 
   518             break :mods .{ mods, char };
   519         };
   520         return try writer.print(
   521             "\x1B[{};{}u",
   522             .{ char, csi_u_mods.seqInt() },
   523         );
   524     }
   525 
   526     // If we have alt-pressed and alt-esc-prefix is enabled, then
   527     // we need to prefix the utf8 sequence with an esc.
   528     if (try legacyAltPrefix(
   529         writer,
   530         event,
   531         binding_mods,
   532         all_mods,
   533         opts,
   534     )) return;
   535 
   536     // If we are on macOS, command+keys do not encode text. It isn't
   537     // typical for command+keys on macOS to ever encode text. They
   538     // don't in native text inputs (i.e. TextEdit) and they also don't
   539     // in other native terminals (Terminal.app officially but also
   540     // iTerm2).
   541     //
   542     // For Linux, we continue to encode text because it is typical.
   543     // For example on Gnome Console Super+b will encode a "b" character
   544     // with legacy encoding.
   545     if ((comptime builtin.target.os.tag == .macos) and all_mods.super) {
   546         return;
   547     }
   548 
   549     return try writer.writeAll(utf8);
   550 }

   557     opts: Options,
   558 ) std.Io.Writer.Error!bool {
   559     // This only takes effect with alt pressed
   560     if (!binding_mods.alt or !opts.alt_esc_prefix) return false;

   562     // On macOS, we only handle option like alt in certain
   563     // circumstances. Otherwise, macOS does a unicode translation
   564     // and we allow that to happen.
   565     if (comptime builtin.target.os.tag == .macos) {
   566         switch (opts.macos_option_as_alt) {
   567             .false => return false,
   568             .left => if (mods.sides.alt == .right) return false,
   569             .right => if (mods.sides.alt == .left) return false,
   570             .true => {},
   571         }
   572     }
   573 
   574     // A single byte is already the exact text we want to prefix. In
   575     // particular, this preserves shifted ASCII punctuation.
   576     const utf8 = event.utf8;
   577     if (utf8.len == 1) {
   578         try writer.writeByte(0x1B);
   579         try writer.writeAll(utf8);
   580         return true;
   581     }
   582 
   583     var unshifted_buf: [4]u8 = undefined;
   584     const value: []const u8 = value: {
   585         // On macOS, Option may translate the text into a different Unicode
   586         // value. When Option is configured as Alt, use the physical key's
   587         // unshifted codepoint just as the single-byte implementation did.

   591                     event.unshifted_codepoint,
   592                     &unshifted_buf,
   593                 ) catch return false;
   594                 break :value unshifted_buf[0..len];
   595             }
   596         }
   597 
   598         // Outside of the macOS translation case, prefix the complete UTF-8
   599         // text rather than truncating it to a single byte.
   600         if (utf8.len > 0) break :value utf8;
   601

   605             const len = std.unicode.utf8Encode(
   606                 event.unshifted_codepoint,
   607                 &unshifted_buf,
   608             ) catch return false;
   609             break :value unshifted_buf[0..len];
   610         }
   611 
   612         return false;
   613     };
   614

   616     try writer.writeAll(value);
