n25 src/input/key_encode.zig:631–699 DIU
   631 fn pcStyleFunctionKey(
   632     keyval: key.Key,
   633     mods: key.Mods,
   634     cursor_key_application: bool,
   635     keypad_key_application_req: bool,
   636     ignore_keypad_with_numlock: bool,
   637     modify_other_keys: bool, // True if state 2
   638     backarrow_key_mode: bool,
   639 ) ?[]const u8 {
   640     // We only want binding-sensitive mods because lock keys
   641     // and directional modifiers (left/right) don't matter for
   642     // pc-style function keys.
   643     const mods_int = mods.binding().int();
   644 
   645     // Keypad application keymode isn't super straightforward.
   646     // On xterm, in VT220 mode, numlock alone is enough to trigger
   647     // application mode. But in more modern modes, numlock is
   648     // ignored by default via mode 1035 (default true). If mode
   649     // 1035 is on, we always are in numerical keypad mode. If
   650     // mode 1035 is off, we are in application mode if the
   651     // proper numlock state is pressed. The numlock state is implicitly
   652     // determined based on the keycode sent (i.e. 1 with numlock
   653     // on will be kp_end).
   654     const keypad_key_application = keypad: {
   655         // If we're ignoring keypad then this is always false.
   656         // In other words, we're always in numerical keypad mode.
   657         if (ignore_keypad_with_numlock) break :keypad false;
   658 
   659         // If we're not ignoring then we enable the desired state.
   660         break :keypad keypad_key_application_req;
   661     };
   662 
   663     for (function_keys.keys.get(keyval)) |entry| {
   664         switch (entry.cursor) {
   665             .any => {},
   666             .normal => if (cursor_key_application) continue,
   667             .application => if (!cursor_key_application) continue,
   668         }
   669 
   670         switch (entry.keypad) {
   671             .any => {},
   672             .normal => if (keypad_key_application) continue,
   673             .application => if (!keypad_key_application) continue,
   674         }
   675 
   676         switch (entry.modify_other_keys) {
   677             .any => {},
   678             .set => if (modify_other_keys) continue,
   679             .set_other => if (!modify_other_keys) continue,
   680         }
   681 
   682         const entry_mods_int = entry.mods.int();
   683         if (entry_mods_int == 0) {
   684             if (mods_int != 0 and !entry.mods_empty_is_any) continue;
   685             // mods are either empty, or empty means any so we allow it.
   686         } else if (entry_mods_int != mods_int) {
   687             // any set mods require an exact match
   688             continue;
   689         }
   690 
   691         if (backarrow_key_mode)
   692             if (entry.sequence_decbkm) |sequence|
   693                 return sequence;
   694 
   695         return entry.sequence;
   696     }
   697 
   698     return null;
   699 }

n25 src/input/key_encode.zig:708–892 DIU
   708 fn ctrlSeq(
   709     logical_key: key.Key,
   710     utf8: []const u8,
   711     unshifted_codepoint: u21,
   712     mods: key.Mods,
   713 ) ?u8 {
   714     const ctrl_only = comptime (key.Mods{ .ctrl = true }).int();
   715 
   716     // If ctrl is not pressed then we never do anything.
   717     if (!mods.ctrl) return null;
   718 
   719     const char, const unset_mods = unset_mods: {
   720         // We need to only get binding modifiers so we strip lock
   721         // keys, sides, etc.
   722         var unset_mods = mods.binding();
   723 
   724         // Remove alt from our modifiers because it does not impact whether
   725         // we are generating a ctrl sequence and we handle the ESC-prefix
   726         // logic separately.
   727         unset_mods.alt = false;
   728 
   729         var char: u8 = char: {
   730             // If we have exactly one UTF8 byte, we assume that is the
   731             // character we want to convert to a C0 byte.
   732             if (utf8.len == 1) break :char utf8[0];
   733 
   734             // If we have a logical key that maps to a single byte
   735             // printable character, we use that. History to explain this:
   736             // this was added to support cyrillic keyboard layouts such
   737             // as Russian and Mongolian. These layouts have a `c` key that
   738             // maps to U+0441 (cyrillic small letter "c") but every
   739             // terminal I've tested encodes this as ctrl+c.
   740             if (logical_key.codepoint()) |cp| {
   741                 if (std.math.cast(u8, cp)) |byte| {
   742                     // For this specific case, we only map to the key if
   743                     // we have exactly ctrl pressed. This is because shift
   744                     // would modify the key and we don't know how to do that
   745                     // properly here (don't have the layout). And we want
   746                     // to encode shift as CSIu.
   747                     if (unset_mods.int() != ctrl_only) return null;
   748                     break :char byte;
   749                 }
   750             }
   751 
   752             // Otherwise we don't have a character to convert that
   753             // we can reliably map to a C0 byte.
   754             return null;
   755         };
   756 
   757         // Remove shift if we have something outside of the US letter
   758         // range. This is so that characters such as `ctrl+shift+-`
   759         // generate the correct ctrl-seq (used by emacs).
   760         if (unset_mods.shift and (char < 'A' or char > 'Z')) shift: {
   761             // Special case for fixterms awkward case as specified.
   762             if (char == '@') break :shift;
   763             unset_mods.shift = false;
   764         }
   765 
   766         // If the character is uppercase, we convert it to lowercase. We
   767         // rely on the unshifted codepoint to do this. This handles
   768         // the scenario where we have caps lock pressed. Note that
   769         // shifted characters are handled above, if we are just pressing
   770         // shift then the ctrl-only check will fail later and we won't
   771         // ctrl-seq encode.
   772         if (char >= 'A' and char <= 'Z' and unshifted_codepoint > 0) {
   773             if (std.math.cast(u8, unshifted_codepoint)) |byte| {
   774                 char = byte;
   775             }
   776         }
   777 
   778         // An additional note on caps lock and shift interaction.
   779         // If we have caps lock set and an ASCII letter is pressed,
   780         // we lowercase it (above). If we have only control pressed,
   781         // we process it as a ctrl seq. For example ctrl+M with caps
   782         // lock but no shift will encode as 0x0D.
   783         //
   784         // But, if you press ctrl+shift+m, this will not encode as a
   785         // ctrl-seq and falls through to CSIu encoding. This lets programs
   786         // detect the difference between ctrl+M and ctrl+shift+M. This
   787         // diverges from the fixterms "spec" and most terminals. This
   788         // only matches Kitty in behavior. But I believe this is a
   789         // justified divergence because it's a useful distinction.
   790 
   791         break :unset_mods .{ char, unset_mods };
   792     };
   793 
   794     // After unsetting, we only continue if we have ONLY control set.
   795     if (unset_mods.int() != ctrl_only) return null;
   796 
   797     // From Kitty's key encoding logic. I tried to discern the exact
   798     // behavior across different terminals but it's not clear, so I'm
   799     // just going to repeat what Kitty does.
   800     return switch (char) {
   801         ' ' => 0,
   802         '/' => 31,
   803         '0' => 48,
   804         '1' => 49,
   805         '2' => 0,
   806         '3' => 27,
   807         '4' => 28,
   808         '5' => 29,
   809         '6' => 30,
   810         '7' => 31,
   811         '8' => 127,
   812         '9' => 57,
   813         '?' => 127,
   814         '@' => 0,
   815         '\\' => 28,
   816         ']' => 29,
   817         '^' => 30,
   818         '_' => 31,
   819         'a' => 1,
   820         'b' => 2,
   821         'c' => 3,
   822         'd' => 4,
   823         'e' => 5,
   824         'f' => 6,
   825         'g' => 7,
   826         'h' => 8,
   827         'j' => 10,
   828         'k' => 11,
   829         'l' => 12,
   830         'n' => 14,
   831         'o' => 15,
   832         'p' => 16,
   833         'q' => 17,
   834         'r' => 18,
   835         's' => 19,
   836         't' => 20,
   837         'u' => 21,
   838         'v' => 22,
   839         'w' => 23,
   840         'x' => 24,
   841         'y' => 25,
   842         'z' => 26,
   843         '~' => 30,
   844 
   845         // These are purposely NOT handled here because of the fixterms
   846         // specification: https://www.leonerd.org.uk/hacks/fixterms/
   847         // These are processed as CSI u.
   848         // 'i' => 0x09,
   849         // 'm' => 0x0D,
   850         // '[' => 0x1B,
   851 
   852         else => null,
   853     };
   854 }
   855 
   856 /// Returns true if this is an ASCII control character, matches libc implementation.
   857 fn isControl(cp: u21) bool {
   858     return cp < 0x20 or cp == 0x7F;
   859 }
   860 
   861 /// Returns true if this string is comprised of a single
   862 /// control character. This returns false for multi-byte strings.
   863 fn isControlUtf8(str: []const u8) bool {
   864     return str.len == 1 and isControl(@intCast(str[0]));
   865 }
   866 
   867 /// This is the bitmask for fixterm CSI u modifiers.
   868 const CsiUMods = packed struct(u3) {
   869     shift: bool = false,
   870     alt: bool = false,
   871     ctrl: bool = false,
   872 
   873     /// Convert an input mods value into the CSI u mods value.
   874     pub fn fromInput(mods: key.Mods) CsiUMods {
   875         return .{
   876             .shift = mods.shift,
   877             .alt = mods.alt,
   878             .ctrl = mods.ctrl,
   879         };
   880     }
   881 
   882     /// Returns the raw int value of this packed struct.
   883     pub fn int(self: CsiUMods) u3 {
   884         return @bitCast(self);
   885     }
   886 
   887     /// Returns the integer value sent as part of the CSI u sequence.
   888     /// This adds 1 to the bitmask value as described in the spec.
   889     pub fn seqInt(self: CsiUMods) u4 {
   890         const raw: u4 = @intCast(self.int());
   891         return raw + 1;
   892     }

n25 src/input/key_encode.zig:924–975 DI
   924 const KittyMods = packed struct(u8) {
   925     shift: bool = false,
   926     alt: bool = false,
   927     ctrl: bool = false,
   928     super: bool = false,
   929     hyper: bool = false,
   930     meta: bool = false,
   931     caps_lock: bool = false,
   932     num_lock: bool = false,
   933 
   934     /// Convert an input mods value into the CSI u mods value.
   935     pub fn fromInput(
   936         action: key.Action,
   937         k: key.Key,
   938         mods: key.Mods,
   939     ) KittyMods {
   940         _ = action;
   941         _ = k;
   942         return .{
   943             .shift = mods.shift,
   944             .alt = mods.alt,
   945             .ctrl = mods.ctrl,
   946             .super = mods.super,
   947             .caps_lock = mods.caps_lock,
   948             .num_lock = mods.num_lock,
   949         };
   950     }
   951 
   952     /// Returns true if the modifiers prevent printable text.
   953     ///
   954     /// The alt_prevents_text parameter determines whether or not the Alt
   955     /// modifier prevents printable text. On Linux, this is always true. On
   956     /// macOS, this is only true if macos-option-as-alt is set.
   957     pub fn preventsText(self: KittyMods, alt_prevents_text: bool) bool {
   958         return (self.alt and alt_prevents_text) or
   959             self.ctrl or
   960             self.super or
   961             self.hyper or
   962             self.meta;
   963     }
   964 
   965     /// Returns the raw int value of this packed struct.
   966     pub fn int(self: KittyMods) u8 {
   967         return @bitCast(self);
   968     }
   969 
   970     /// Returns the integer value sent as part of the Kitty sequence.
   971     /// This adds 1 to the bitmask value as described in the spec.
   972     pub fn seqInt(self: KittyMods) u9 {
   973         const raw: u9 = @intCast(self.int());
   974         return raw + 1;
   975     }

n25 src/input/key_encode.zig:1010–1117 DIU
  1010 const KittySequence = struct {
  1011     key: u21,
  1012     final: u8,
  1013     mods: KittyMods = .{},
  1014     event: Event = .none,
  1015     alternates: [2]?u21 = .{ null, null },
  1016     text: []const u8 = "",
  1017 
  1018     /// Values for the event code (see "event-type" in above comment).
  1019     /// Note that Kitty omits the ":1" for the press event but other
  1020     /// terminals include it. We'll include it.
  1021     const Event = enum(u2) {
  1022         none = 0,
  1023         press = 1,
  1024         repeat = 2,
  1025         release = 3,
  1026     };
  1027 
  1028     pub fn encode(
  1029         self: KittySequence,
  1030         writer: *std.Io.Writer,
  1031     ) std.Io.Writer.Error!void {
  1032         if (self.final == 'u' or self.final == '~') return try self.encodeFull(writer);
  1033         return try self.encodeSpecial(writer);
  1034     }
  1035 
  1036     fn encodeFull(
  1037         self: KittySequence,
  1038         writer: *std.Io.Writer,
  1039     ) std.Io.Writer.Error!void {
  1040         // Key section
  1041         try writer.print("\x1B[{d}", .{self.key});
  1042         // Write our alternates
  1043         if (self.alternates[0]) |shifted| try writer.print(":{d}", .{shifted});
  1044         if (self.alternates[1]) |base| {
  1045             if (self.alternates[0] == null) {
  1046                 try writer.print("::{d}", .{base});
  1047             } else {
  1048                 try writer.print(":{d}", .{base});
  1049             }
  1050         }
  1051 
  1052         // Mods and events section
  1053         const mods = self.mods.seqInt();
  1054         var emit_prior = false;
  1055         if (self.event != .none and self.event != .press) {
  1056             try writer.print(
  1057                 ";{d}:{d}",
  1058                 .{ mods, @backingInt(self.event) },
  1059             );
  1060             emit_prior = true;
  1061         } else if (mods > 1) {
  1062             try writer.print(";{d}", .{mods});
  1063             emit_prior = true;
  1064         }
  1065 
  1066         // Text section
  1067         if (self.text.len > 0) text: {
  1068             const view = std.unicode.Utf8View.init(self.text) catch {
  1069                 // Assume invalid UTF-8 means we have no text.
  1070                 break :text;
  1071             };
  1072             var it = view.iterator();
  1073             var count: usize = 0;
  1074             while (it.nextCodepoint()) |cp| {
  1075                 // If the codepoint is non-printable ASCII character, skip.
  1076                 if (isControl(cp)) continue;
  1077 
  1078                 // We need to add our ";". We need to add two if we didn't emit
  1079                 // the modifier section. We only do this initially.
  1080                 if (count == 0) {
  1081                     if (!emit_prior) try writer.writeByte(';');
  1082                     try writer.writeByte(';');
  1083                 } else {
  1084                     try writer.writeByte(':');
  1085                 }
  1086 
  1087                 try writer.print("{d}", .{cp});
  1088                 count += 1;
  1089             }
  1090         }
  1091 
  1092         try writer.print("{c}", .{self.final});
  1093     }
  1094 
  1095     fn encodeSpecial(
  1096         self: KittySequence,
  1097         writer: *std.Io.Writer,
  1098     ) std.Io.Writer.Error!void {
  1099         const mods = self.mods.seqInt();
  1100         if (self.event != .none) {
  1101             return try writer.print("\x1B[1;{d}:{d}{c}", .{
  1102                 mods,
  1103                 @backingInt(self.event),
  1104                 self.final,
  1105             });
  1106         }
  1107 
  1108         if (mods > 1) {
  1109             return try writer.print("\x1B[1;{d}{c}", .{
  1110                 mods,
  1111                 self.final,
  1112             });
  1113         }
  1114 
  1115         return try writer.print("\x1B[{c}", .{self.final});
  1116     }
  1117 };

    27 };

n25 src/terminal/kitty/key.zig:5–62 DI
     5 /// Stack for the key flags. This implements the push/pop behavior
     6 /// of the CSI > u and CSI < u sequences. We implement the stack as
     7 /// fixed size to avoid heap allocation.
     8 pub const FlagStack = struct {
     9     const len = 8;
    10 
    11     flags: [len]Flags = @splat(.disabled),
    12     idx: u3 = 0,
    13 
    14     /// Return the current stack value
    15     pub fn current(self: FlagStack) Flags {
    16         return self.flags[self.idx];
    17     }
    18 
    19     /// Perform the "set" operation as described in the spec for
    20     /// the CSI = u sequence.
    21     pub fn set(
    22         self: *FlagStack,
    23         mode: SetMode,
    24         v: Flags,
    25     ) void {
    26         switch (mode) {
    27             .set => self.flags[self.idx] = v,
    28             .@"or" => self.flags[self.idx] = @bitCast(
    29                 self.flags[self.idx].int() | v.int(),
    30             ),
    31             .not => self.flags[self.idx] = @bitCast(
    32                 self.flags[self.idx].int() & ~v.int(),
    33             ),
    34         }
    35     }
    36 
    37     /// Push a new set of flags onto the stack. If the stack is full
    38     /// then the oldest entry is evicted.
    39     pub fn push(self: *FlagStack, flags: Flags) void {
    40         // Overflow and wrap around if we're full, which evicts
    41         // the oldest entry.
    42         self.idx +%= 1;
    43         self.flags[self.idx] = flags;
    44     }
    45 
    46     /// Pop `n` entries from the stack. This will just wrap around
    47     /// if `n` is greater than the amount in the stack.
    48     pub fn pop(self: *FlagStack, n: usize) void {
    49         // If n is more than our length then we just reset the stack.
    50         // This also avoids a DoS vector where a malicious client
    51         // could send a huge number of pop commands to waste cpu.
    52         if (n >= self.flags.len) {
    53             self.idx = 0;
    54             self.flags = @splat(.disabled);
    55             return;
    56         }
    57 
    58         for (0..n) |_| {
    59             self.flags[self.idx] = .disabled;
    60             self.idx -%= 1;
    61         }
    62     }

    98         .report_events = true,
    99         .report_alternates = true,
   100         .report_all = true,
   101         .report_associated = true,
   102     };
   103

   106     }

   139 fn setPacked(values: *ModePacked, mode: Mode, value: bool) void {
   140     switch (mode) {
   141         inline else => |mode_comptime| {
   142             const entry = comptime entryForMode(mode_comptime);
   143             @field(values, entry.name) = value;
   144         },
   145     }
   146 }
   147 
   148 fn getPacked(values: *const ModePacked, mode: Mode) bool {
   149     switch (mode) {
   150         inline else => |mode_comptime| {
   151             const entry = comptime entryForMode(mode_comptime);
   152             return @field(values, entry.name);
   153         },
   154     }
   155 }

n25 src/terminal/modes.zig:216–231 DI
   216 pub fn modeFromInt(v: u16, ansi: bool) ?Mode {
   217     // ModeTag stores the number in 15 bits. A larger number can't be a
   218     // known mode, and packing it below would spill into the ansi bit.
   219     if (v > std.math.maxInt(u15)) return null;
   220 
   221     // This runs for every mode that is set, reset, or queried, so we use
   222     // a sorted lookup instead of comparing against each entry in turn.
   223     const tag: ModeTag = .{ .ansi = ansi, .value = @intCast(v) };
   224     const int: ModeTag.Backing = @bitCast(tag);
   225     const idx = EntrySet.indexOf(int) orelse return null;
   226     if (comptime entries_any_disabled) {
   227         if (entries_disabled[idx]) return null;
   228     }
   229 
   230     return @fromBackingInt(int);
   231 }

n26 macos/Sources/Features/Terminal/BaseTerminalController.swift:282–316 DIU
   282     func newSplit(
   283         at oldView: Ghostty.SurfaceView,
   284         direction: SplitTree<Ghostty.SurfaceView>.NewDirection,
   285         baseConfig config: Ghostty.SurfaceConfiguration? = nil
   286     ) -> Ghostty.SurfaceView? {
   287         // We can only create new splits for surfaces in our tree.
   288         guard surfaceTree.root?.node(view: oldView) != nil else { return nil }
   289 
   290         // Create a new surface view
   291         guard let ghostty_app = ghostty.app else { return nil }
   292         let newView = Ghostty.SurfaceView(ghostty_app, baseConfig: config)
   293 
   294         // Do the split
   295         let newTree: SplitTree<Ghostty.SurfaceView>
   296         do {
   297             newTree = try surfaceTree.inserting(
   298                 view: newView,
   299                 at: oldView,
   300                 direction: direction)
   301         } catch {
   302             // If splitting fails for any reason (it should not), then we just log
   303             // and return. The new view we created will be deinitialized and its
   304             // no big deal.
   305             Ghostty.logger.warning("failed to insert split: \(error, privacy: .public)")
   306             return nil
   307         }
   308 
   309         replaceSurfaceTree(
   310             newTree,
   311             moveFocusTo: newView,
   312             moveFocusFrom: oldView,
   313             undoAction: "New Split")
   314 
   315         return newView
   316     }

n26 macos/Sources/Features/Terminal/BaseTerminalController.swift:666–688 DIU
   666     @objc private func ghosttyDidNewSplit(_ notification: Notification) {
   667         // The target must be within our tree
   668         guard let oldView = notification.object as? Ghostty.SurfaceView else { return }
   669         guard surfaceTree.root?.node(view: oldView) != nil else { return }
   670 
   671         // Notification must contain our base config
   672         let configAny = notification.userInfo?[Ghostty.Notification.NewSurfaceConfigKey]
   673         let config = configAny as? Ghostty.SurfaceConfiguration
   674 
   675         // Determine our desired direction
   676         guard let directionAny = notification.userInfo?["direction"] else { return }
   677         guard let direction = directionAny as? ghostty_action_split_direction_e else { return }
   678         let splitDirection: SplitTree<Ghostty.SurfaceView>.NewDirection
   679         switch direction {
   680         case GHOSTTY_SPLIT_DIRECTION_RIGHT: splitDirection = .right
   681         case GHOSTTY_SPLIT_DIRECTION_LEFT: splitDirection = .left
   682         case GHOSTTY_SPLIT_DIRECTION_DOWN: splitDirection = .down
   683         case GHOSTTY_SPLIT_DIRECTION_UP: splitDirection = .up
   684         default: return
   685         }
   686 
   687         newSplit(at: oldView, direction: splitDirection, baseConfig: config)
   688     }

   572         static func action(_ app: ghostty_app_t, target: ghostty_target_s, action: ghostty_action_s) -> Bool {
   573             // Make sure it a target we understand so all our action handlers can assert
   574             switch target.tag {
   575             case GHOSTTY_TARGET_APP, GHOSTTY_TARGET_SURFACE:
   576                 break
   577

   581             }

   584             switch action.tag {
   585             case GHOSTTY_ACTION_QUIT:
   586                 quit(app)
   587 
   588             case GHOSTTY_ACTION_NEW_WINDOW:
   589                 newWindow(app, target: target)
   590 
   591             case GHOSTTY_ACTION_NEW_TAB:
   592                 newTab(app, target: target)
   593 
   594             case GHOSTTY_ACTION_NEW_SPLIT:
   595                 newSplit(app, target: target, direction: action.action.new_split)

n26 src/App.zig:444–529 DIU
   444 pub fn performAction(
   445     self: *App,
   446     rt_app: *apprt.App,
   447     action: input.Binding.Action.Scoped(.app),
   448 ) !void {
   449     switch (action) {
   450         .unbind => unreachable,
   451         .ignore => {},
   452         .quit => _ = try rt_app.performAction(.app, .quit, {}),
   453         .new_window => _ = try self.newWindow(rt_app, .{ .parent = null }),
   454         .open_config => |v| _ = try rt_app.performAction(
   455             .app,
   456             .open_config,
   457             switch (v) {
   458                 .os_open => .os_open,
   459                 .new_window => .new_window,
   460             },
   461         ),
   462         .reload_config => _ = try rt_app.performAction(.app, .reload_config, .{}),
   463         .set_window_theme => |v| _ = try rt_app.performAction(
   464             .app,
   465             .set_window_theme,
   466             switch (v) {
   467                 .config => .config,
   468                 .system => .system,
   469                 .light => .light,
   470                 .dark => .dark,
   471             },
   472         ),
   473         .close_all_windows => _ = try rt_app.performAction(.app, .close_all_windows, {}),
   474         .toggle_quick_terminal => _ = try rt_app.performAction(.app, .toggle_quick_terminal, {}),
   475         .toggle_visibility => _ = try rt_app.performAction(.app, .toggle_visibility, {}),
   476         .check_for_updates => _ = try rt_app.performAction(.app, .check_for_updates, {}),
   477         .show_gtk_inspector => _ = try rt_app.performAction(.app, .show_gtk_inspector, {}),
   478         .undo => _ = try rt_app.performAction(.app, .undo, {}),
   479 
   480         .redo => _ = try rt_app.performAction(.app, .redo, {}),
   481     }
   482 }
   483 
   484 /// Performs a chained action. We will continue executing each action
   485 /// even if there is a failure in a prior action.
   486 pub fn performAllChainedAction(
   487     self: *App,
   488     rt_app: *apprt.App,
   489     actions: []const input.Binding.Action,
   490 ) void {
   491     for (actions) |action| {
   492         self.performAllAction(rt_app, action) catch |err| {
   493             log.warn("error performing chained action action={s} err={}", .{
   494                 @tagName(action),
   495                 err,
   496             });
   497         };
   498     }
   499 }
   500 
   501 /// Perform an app-wide binding action. If the action is surface-specific
   502 /// then it will be performed on all surfaces. To perform only app-scoped
   503 /// actions, use performAction.
   504 pub fn performAllAction(
   505     self: *App,
   506     rt_app: *apprt.App,
   507     action: input.Binding.Action,
   508 ) !void {
   509     switch (action.scope()) {
   510         // App-scoped actions are handled by the app so that they aren't
   511         // repeated for each surface (since each surface forwards
   512         // app-scoped actions back up).
   513         .app => try self.performAction(
   514             rt_app,
   515             action.scoped(.app).?, // asserted through the scope match
   516         ),
   517 
   518         // Surface-scoped actions are performed on all surfaces. Errors
   519         // are logged but processing continues.
   520         .surface => for (self.surfaces.items) |surface| {
   521             _ = surface.core().performBindingAction(action) catch |err| {
   522                 log.warn("error performing binding action on surface id={x} err={}", .{
   523                     surface.core().id,
   524                     err,
   525                 });
   526             };
   527         },
   528     }
   529 }

n26 src/Surface.zig:2325–2443 DIU
  2325 fn copySelectionToClipboards(
  2326     self: *Surface,
  2327     sel: terminal.Selection,
  2328     clipboards: []const apprt.Clipboard,
  2329     format: input.Binding.Action.CopyToClipboard,
  2330 ) !void {
  2331     // Create an arena to simplify memory management here.
  2332     var arena = ArenaAllocator.init(self.alloc);
  2333     defer arena.deinit();
  2334     const alloc = arena.allocator();
  2335 
  2336     // The options we'll use for all formatting. We'll just override the
  2337     // emit format.
  2338     const opts: terminal.formatter.Options = .{
  2339         .emit = .plain, // We'll override this below
  2340         .unwrap = true,
  2341         .trim = self.config.clipboard_trim_trailing_spaces,
  2342         .codepoint_map = self.config.clipboard_codepoint_map.map.list,
  2343         .background = self.io.terminal.colors.background.get(),
  2344         .foreground = self.io.terminal.colors.foreground.get(),
  2345         .palette = &self.io.terminal.colors.palette.current,
  2346     };
  2347 
  2348     const ScreenFormatter = terminal.formatter.ScreenFormatter;
  2349     var aw: std.Io.Writer.Allocating = .init(alloc);
  2350     var contents_buf: [2]apprt.ClipboardContent = undefined;
  2351     var contents: std.ArrayList(apprt.ClipboardContent) = .initBuffer(&contents_buf);
  2352     switch (format) {
  2353         .plain => {
  2354             var formatter: ScreenFormatter = .init(self.io.terminal.screens.active, opts);
  2355             formatter.content = .{ .selection = sel };
  2356             try formatter.format(&aw.writer);
  2357             contents.appendAssumeCapacity(.{
  2358                 .mime = "text/plain",
  2359                 .data = try aw.toOwnedSliceSentinel(0),
  2360             });
  2361         },
  2362 
  2363         .vt => {
  2364             var formatter: ScreenFormatter = .init(self.io.terminal.screens.active, opts: {
  2365                 var copy = opts;
  2366                 copy.emit = .vt;
  2367                 break :opts copy;
  2368             });
  2369             formatter.content = .{ .selection = sel };
  2370             try formatter.format(&aw.writer);
  2371 
  2372             // Note: We don't apply codepoint mappings to VT format since it contains
  2373             // escape sequences that should be preserved as-is
  2374             contents.appendAssumeCapacity(.{
  2375                 .mime = "text/plain",
  2376                 .data = try aw.toOwnedSliceSentinel(0),
  2377             });
  2378         },
  2379 
  2380         .html => {
  2381             var formatter: ScreenFormatter = .init(self.io.terminal.screens.active, opts: {
  2382                 var copy = opts;
  2383                 copy.emit = .html;
  2384                 break :opts copy;
  2385             });
  2386             formatter.content = .{ .selection = sel };
  2387             try formatter.format(&aw.writer);
  2388 
  2389             // Note: We don't apply codepoint mappings to HTML format since HTML
  2390             // has its own character encoding and entity system
  2391             contents.appendAssumeCapacity(.{
  2392                 .mime = "text/html",
  2393                 .data = try aw.toOwnedSliceSentinel(0),
  2394             });
  2395         },
  2396 
  2397         .mixed => {
  2398             // First, generate plain text with codepoint mappings applied
  2399             var formatter: ScreenFormatter = .init(self.io.terminal.screens.active, opts);
  2400             formatter.content = .{ .selection = sel };
  2401             try formatter.format(&aw.writer);
  2402             contents.appendAssumeCapacity(.{
  2403                 .mime = "text/plain",
  2404                 .data = try aw.toOwnedSliceSentinel(0),
  2405             });
  2406 
  2407             assert(aw.written().len == 0);
  2408             // Second, generate HTML without codepoint mappings
  2409             formatter = .init(self.io.terminal.screens.active, opts: {
  2410                 var copy = opts;
  2411                 copy.emit = .html;
  2412 
  2413                 // We purposely don't emit background/foreground for mixed
  2414                 // mode because the HTML contents is often used for rich text
  2415                 // input and with trimmed spaces it looks pretty bad.
  2416                 copy.background = null;
  2417                 copy.foreground = null;
  2418 
  2419                 break :opts copy;
  2420             });
  2421             formatter.content = .{ .selection = sel };
  2422             try formatter.format(&aw.writer);
  2423 
  2424             // Note: We don't apply codepoint mappings to HTML format
  2425             contents.appendAssumeCapacity(.{
  2426                 .mime = "text/html",
  2427                 .data = try aw.toOwnedSliceSentinel(0),
  2428             });
  2429         },
  2430     }
  2431 
  2432     assert(contents.items.len > 0);
  2433     for (clipboards) |clipboard| self.rt_surface.setClipboard(
  2434         clipboard,
  2435         contents.items,
  2436         false,
  2437     ) catch |err| {
  2438         log.err(
  2439             "error setting clipboard string clipboard={} err={}",
  2440             .{ clipboard, err },
  2441         );
  2442     };
  2443 }

n26 src/Surface.zig:2451–2473 DIU
  2451 fn setSelection(self: *Surface, sel_: ?terminal.Selection) !void {
  2452     // Compute the transition before `select` below, which untracks (frees)
  2453     // the previous selection's tracked pins; reading them after would be a
  2454     // use-after-free.
  2455     const prev_ = self.io.terminal.screens.active.selection;
  2456     const changed = changed: {
  2457         const prev = prev_ orelse break :changed sel_ != null;
  2458         const sel = sel_ orelse break :changed true;
  2459         break :changed !sel.eql(prev);
  2460     };
  2461 
  2462     try self.io.terminal.screens.active.select(sel_);
  2463 
  2464     if (changed) {
  2465         _ = self.rt_app.performAction(
  2466             .{ .surface = self },
  2467             .selection_changed,
  2468             {},
  2469         ) catch |err| {
  2470             log.warn("apprt failed selection_changed notification err={}", .{err});
  2471         };
  2472     }
  2473 }

n26 src/Surface.zig:3129–3157 IU
  3129     const performed = performed: {
  3130         // If this is a global or all action, then we perform it on
  3131         // the app and it applies to every surface.
  3132         if (leaf.flags.global or leaf.flags.all) {
  3133             self.app.performAllChainedAction(
  3134                 self.rt_app,
  3135                 actions,
  3136             );
  3137 
  3138             // "All" actions are always performed since they are global.
  3139             break :performed true;
  3140         }
  3141 
  3142         // Perform each action. We are performed if ANY of the chained
  3143         // actions perform.
  3144         var performed: bool = false;
  3145         for (actions) |action| {
  3146             if (self.performBindingAction(action)) |v| {
  3147                 performed = performed or v;
  3148             } else |err| {
  3149                 log.info(
  3150                     "key binding action failed action={t} err={}",
  3151                     .{ action, err },
  3152                 );
  3153             }
  3154         }
  3155 
  3156         break :performed performed;
  3157     };

n26 src/Surface.zig:4941–5819 DIU
  4941 pub fn performBindingAction(self: *Surface, action: input.Binding.Action) !bool {
  4942     // Forward app-scoped actions to the app. Some app-scoped actions are
  4943     // special-cased here because they do some special things when performed
  4944     // from the surface.
  4945     if (action.scoped(.app)) |app_action| {
  4946         switch (app_action) {
  4947             .new_window => try self.app.newWindow(
  4948                 self.rt_app,
  4949                 .{ .parent = self },
  4950             ),
  4951 
  4952             // Undo and redo both support both surface and app targeting.
  4953             // If we are triggering on a surface then we perform the
  4954             // action with the surface target.
  4955             .undo => return try self.rt_app.performAction(
  4956                 .{ .surface = self },
  4957                 .undo,
  4958                 {},
  4959             ),
  4960 
  4961             .redo => return try self.rt_app.performAction(
  4962                 .{ .surface = self },
  4963                 .redo,
  4964                 {},
  4965             ),
  4966 
  4967             else => try self.app.performAction(
  4968                 self.rt_app,
  4969                 action.scoped(.app).?,
  4970             ),
  4971         }
  4972         return true;
  4973     }
  4974 
  4975     switch (action.scoped(.surface).?) {
  4976         .csi, .esc => |data| {
  4977             // We need to send the CSI/ESC sequence as a single write request.
  4978             // If you split it across two then the shell can interpret it
  4979             // as two literals.
  4980             var buf: [128]u8 = undefined;
  4981             const full_data = switch (action) {
  4982                 .csi => try std.mem.print(&buf, "\x1b[{s}", .{data}),
  4983                 .esc => try std.mem.print(&buf, "\x1b{s}", .{data}),
  4984                 else => unreachable,
  4985             };
  4986             self.queueIo(try termio.Message.writeReq(
  4987                 self.alloc,
  4988                 full_data,
  4989             ), .unlocked);
  4990 
  4991             // CSI/ESC triggers a scroll.
  4992             {
  4993                 self.renderer_state.mutex.lockUncancelable(global.io());
  4994                 defer self.renderer_state.mutex.unlock(global.io());
  4995                 self.scrollToBottom() catch |err| {
  4996                     log.warn("error scrolling to bottom err={}", .{err});
  4997                 };
  4998             }
  4999         },
  5000 
  5001         .text => |data| {
  5002             var stack_buf: [256]u8 = undefined;
  5003             var stack: std.heap.BufferFirstAllocator = .init(&stack_buf, self.alloc);
  5004             const alloc = stack.allocator();
  5005             const buf = try alloc.alloc(u8, data.len);
  5006             defer alloc.free(buf);
  5007             const text = configpkg.string.parse(buf, data) catch |err| {
  5008                 log.warn(
  5009                     "error parsing text binding text={s} err={}",
  5010                     .{ data, err },
  5011                 );
  5012                 return true;
  5013             };
  5014             self.queueIo(try termio.Message.writeReq(
  5015                 self.alloc,
  5016                 text,
  5017             ), .unlocked);
  5018 
  5019             // Text triggers a scroll.
  5020             {
  5021                 self.renderer_state.mutex.lockUncancelable(global.io());
  5022                 defer self.renderer_state.mutex.unlock(global.io());
  5023                 self.scrollToBottom() catch |err| {
  5024                     log.warn("error scrolling to bottom err={}", .{err});
  5025                 };
  5026             }
  5027         },
  5028 
  5029         .cursor_key => |ck| {
  5030             // We send a different sequence depending on if we're
  5031             // in cursor keys mode. We're in "normal" mode if cursor
  5032             // keys mode is NOT set.
  5033             const normal = normal: {
  5034                 self.renderer_state.mutex.lockUncancelable(global.io());
  5035                 defer self.renderer_state.mutex.unlock(global.io());
  5036 
  5037                 // With the lock held, we must scroll to the bottom.
  5038                 // We always scroll to the bottom for these inputs.
  5039                 self.scrollToBottom() catch |err| {
  5040                     log.warn("error scrolling to bottom err={}", .{err});
  5041                 };
  5042 
  5043                 break :normal !self.io.terminal.modes.get(.cursor_keys);
  5044             };
  5045 
  5046             if (normal) {
  5047                 self.queueIo(.{ .write_stable = ck.normal }, .unlocked);
  5048             } else {
  5049                 self.queueIo(.{ .write_stable = ck.application }, .unlocked);
  5050             }
  5051         },
  5052 
  5053         .reset => {
  5054             self.renderer_state.mutex.lockUncancelable(global.io());
  5055             defer self.renderer_state.mutex.unlock(global.io());
  5056             self.renderer_state.terminal.fullReset();
  5057         },
  5058 
  5059         .start_search => {
  5060             // To save resources, we don't actually start a search here,
  5061             // we just notify the apprt. The real thread will start when
  5062             // the first needles are set.
  5063             return try self.rt_app.performAction(
  5064                 .{ .surface = self },
  5065                 .start_search,
  5066                 .{ .needle = "" },
  5067             );
  5068         },
  5069 
  5070         .search_selection => {
  5071             const selection = try self.selectionString(self.alloc) orelse return false;
  5072             defer self.alloc.free(selection);
  5073             return try self.rt_app.performAction(
  5074                 .{ .surface = self },
  5075                 .start_search,
  5076                 .{ .needle = selection },
  5077             );
  5078         },
  5079 
  5080         .end_search => {
  5081             // We only return that this was performed if we actually
  5082             // stopped a search, but we also send the apprt end_search so
  5083             // that GUIs can clean up stale stuff.
  5084             const performed = self.search != null;
  5085 
  5086             if (self.search) |*s| {
  5087                 s.deinit();
  5088                 self.search = null;
  5089             }
  5090 
  5091             _ = try self.rt_app.performAction(
  5092                 .{ .surface = self },
  5093                 .end_search,
  5094                 {},
  5095             );
  5096 
  5097             return performed;
  5098         },
  5099 
  5100         .search => |text| search: {
  5101             const s: *Search = if (self.search) |*s| s else init: {
  5102                 // If we're stopping the search and we had no prior search,
  5103                 // then there is nothing to do.
  5104                 if (text.len == 0) return false;
  5105 
  5106                 // We need to assign directly to self.search because we need
  5107                 // a stable pointer back to the thread state.
  5108                 self.search = .{
  5109                     .state = try .init(self.alloc, .{
  5110                         .mutex = self.renderer_state.mutex,
  5111                         .terminal = self.renderer_state.terminal,
  5112                         .event_cb = &searchCallback,
  5113                         .event_userdata = self,
  5114                     }),
  5115                     .thread = undefined,
  5116                 };
  5117                 const s: *Search = &self.search.?;
  5118                 errdefer s.state.deinit();
  5119 
  5120                 s.thread = try .spawn(
  5121                     .{},
  5122                     terminal.search.Thread.threadMain,
  5123                     .{&s.state},
  5124                 );
  5125                 s.thread.setName(global.io(), "search") catch {};
  5126 
  5127                 break :init s;
  5128             };
  5129 
  5130             // Zero-length text means stop searching.
  5131             if (text.len == 0) {
  5132                 s.deinit();
  5133                 self.search = null;
  5134                 break :search;
  5135             }
  5136 
  5137             _ = s.state.mailbox.push(
  5138                 global.io(),
  5139                 .{ .change_needle = try .init(
  5140                     self.alloc,
  5141                     text,
  5142                 ) },
  5143                 .forever,
  5144             );
  5145             s.state.wakeup.notify() catch {};
  5146         },
  5147 
  5148         .navigate_search => |nav| {
  5149             const s: *Search = if (self.search) |*s| s else return false;
  5150             _ = s.state.mailbox.push(
  5151                 global.io(),
  5152                 .{ .select = switch (nav) {
  5153                     .next => .next,
  5154                     .previous => .prev,
  5155                 } },
  5156                 .forever,
  5157             );
  5158             s.state.wakeup.notify() catch {};
  5159         },
  5160 
  5161         .copy_to_clipboard => |format| {
  5162             self.renderer_state.mutex.lockUncancelable(global.io());
  5163             defer self.renderer_state.mutex.unlock(global.io());
  5164 
  5165             if (self.io.terminal.screens.active.selection) |sel| {
  5166                 try self.copySelectionToClipboards(
  5167                     sel,
  5168                     &.{.standard},
  5169                     format,
  5170                 );
  5171 
  5172                 // Clear the selection if configured to do so.
  5173                 if (self.config.selection_clear_on_copy) {
  5174                     if (self.setSelection(null)) {
  5175                         self.queueRender() catch |err| {
  5176                             log.warn("failed to queue render after clear selection err={}", .{err});
  5177                         };
  5178                     } else |err| {
  5179                         log.warn("failed to clear selection after copy err={}", .{err});
  5180                     }
  5181                 }
  5182 
  5183                 return true;
  5184             }
  5185 
  5186             return false;
  5187         },
  5188 
  5189         .copy_url_to_clipboard => {
  5190             // If the mouse isn't over a link, nothing we can do.
  5191             if (!self.mouse.over_link) return false;
  5192             const pos = try self.rt_surface.getCursorPos();
  5193 
  5194             self.renderer_state.mutex.lockUncancelable(global.io());
  5195             defer self.renderer_state.mutex.unlock(global.io());
  5196             if (try self.linkAtPos(pos)) |link_info| {
  5197                 const url_text = switch (link_info.action) {
  5198                     .open => url_text: {
  5199                         // For regex links, get the text from selection
  5200                         break :url_text (self.io.terminal.screens.active.selectionString(self.alloc, .{
  5201                             .sel = link_info.selection,
  5202                             .trim = self.config.clipboard_trim_trailing_spaces,
  5203                         })) catch |err| {
  5204                             log.err("error reading url string err={}", .{err});
  5205                             return false;
  5206                         };
  5207                     },
  5208 
  5209                     ._open_osc8 => url_text: {
  5210                         // For OSC8 links, get the URI directly from hyperlink data
  5211                         const uri = self.osc8URI(link_info.selection.start()) orelse {
  5212                             log.warn("failed to get URI for OSC8 hyperlink", .{});
  5213                             return false;
  5214                         };
  5215                         break :url_text try self.alloc.dupeSentinel(u8, uri, 0);
  5216                     },
  5217                 };
  5218                 defer self.alloc.free(url_text);
  5219 
  5220                 self.rt_surface.setClipboard(.standard, &.{.{
  5221                     .mime = "text/plain",
  5222                     .data = url_text,
  5223                 }}, false) catch |err| {
  5224                     log.err("error copying url to clipboard err={}", .{err});
  5225                     return false;
  5226                 };
  5227 
  5228                 return true;
  5229             }
  5230 
  5231             return false;
  5232         },
  5233 
  5234         .copy_title_to_clipboard => return try self.rt_app.performAction(
  5235             .{ .surface = self },
  5236             .copy_title_to_clipboard,
  5237             {},
  5238         ),
  5239

  5250         .increase_font_size => |delta| {
  5251             // Max delta is somewhat arbitrary.
  5252             const clamped_delta = @max(0, @min(255, delta));
  5253 
  5254             log.debug("increase font size={}", .{clamped_delta});
  5255 
  5256             // Max point size is somewhat arbitrary.
  5257             var size = self.font_size;
  5258             size.points = @min(size.points + clamped_delta, 255);
  5259             try self.setFontSize(size);
  5260 
  5261             // Mark that we manually adjusted the font size
  5262             self.font_size_adjusted = true;
  5263         },
  5264 
  5265         .decrease_font_size => |delta| {
  5266             // Max delta is somewhat arbitrary.
  5267             const clamped_delta = @max(0, @min(255, delta));
  5268 
  5269             log.debug("decrease font size={}", .{clamped_delta});
  5270 
  5271             var size = self.font_size;
  5272             size.points = @max(1, size.points - clamped_delta);
  5273             try self.setFontSize(size);
  5274 
  5275             // Mark that we manually adjusted the font size
  5276             self.font_size_adjusted = true;
  5277         },
  5278 
  5279         .reset_font_size => {
  5280             log.debug("reset font size", .{});
  5281 
  5282             var size = self.font_size;
  5283             size.points = self.config.original_font_size;
  5284             try self.setFontSize(size);
  5285 
  5286             // Reset font size also resets the manual adjustment state
  5287             self.font_size_adjusted = false;
  5288         },
  5289 
  5290         .set_font_size => |points| {
  5291             log.debug("set font size={d}", .{points});
  5292 
  5293             var size = self.font_size;
  5294             size.points = std.math.clamp(points, 1.0, 255.0);
  5295             try self.setFontSize(size);
  5296 
  5297             // Mark that we manually adjusted the font size
  5298             self.font_size_adjusted = true;
  5299         },
  5300

  5349         .clear_screen => {
  5350             // This is a duplicate of some of the logic in termio.clearScreen
  5351             // but we need to do this here so we can know the answer before
  5352             // we send the message. If the currently active screen is on the
  5353             // alternate screen then clear screen does nothing so we want to
  5354             // return false so the keybind can be unconsumed.
  5355             {
  5356                 self.renderer_state.mutex.lockUncancelable(global.io());
  5357                 defer self.renderer_state.mutex.unlock(global.io());
  5358                 if (self.io.terminal.screens.active_key == .alternate) return false;
  5359             }
  5360 
  5361             self.queueIo(.{
  5362                 .clear_screen = .{ .history = true },
  5363             }, .unlocked);
  5364         },
  5365 
  5366         .scroll_to_top => {
  5367             self.queueIo(.{
  5368                 .scroll_viewport = .{ .top = {} },

  5370         },

  5372         .scroll_to_bottom => {

  5374                 .scroll_viewport = .{ .bottom = {} },

  5378         .scroll_to_row => |n| {
  5379             {
  5380                 self.renderer_state.mutex.lockUncancelable(global.io());
  5381                 defer self.renderer_state.mutex.unlock(global.io());
  5382                 const t: *terminal.Terminal = self.renderer_state.terminal;
  5383                 t.screens.active.scroll(.{ .row = n });
  5384             }
  5385 
  5386             try self.queueRender();
  5387         },
  5388 
  5389         .scroll_to_selection => {
  5390             {
  5391                 self.renderer_state.mutex.lockUncancelable(global.io());
  5392                 defer self.renderer_state.mutex.unlock(global.io());
  5393                 const sel = self.io.terminal.screens.active.selection orelse return false;
  5394                 const tl = sel.topLeft(self.io.terminal.screens.active);
  5395                 self.io.terminal.screens.active.scroll(.{ .pin = tl });
  5396             }
  5397 
  5398             try self.queueRender();
  5399         },
  5400 
  5401         .scroll_page_up => {
  5402             const rows: isize = @intCast(self.size.grid().rows);
  5403             self.queueIo(.{
  5404                 .scroll_viewport = .{ .delta = -1 * rows },
  5405             }, .unlocked);
  5406         },
  5407 
  5408         .scroll_page_down => {
  5409             const rows: isize = @intCast(self.size.grid().rows);
  5410             self.queueIo(.{
  5411                 .scroll_viewport = .{ .delta = rows },
  5412             }, .unlocked);
  5413         },
  5414 
  5415         .scroll_page_fractional => |fraction| {
  5416             const rows: f32 = @floatFromInt(self.size.grid().rows);
  5417             const delta: isize = @intFromFloat(@trunc(fraction * rows));
  5418             self.queueIo(.{
  5419                 .scroll_viewport = .{ .delta = delta },
  5420             }, .unlocked);
  5421         },
  5422

  5629         .show_on_screen_keyboard => return try self.rt_app.performAction(
  5630             .{ .surface = self },
  5631             .show_on_screen_keyboard,
  5632             {},
  5633         ),
  5634 
  5635         .select_all => {
  5636             self.renderer_state.mutex.lockUncancelable(global.io());
  5637             defer self.renderer_state.mutex.unlock(global.io());
  5638 
  5639             const sel = self.io.terminal.screens.active.selectAll();
  5640             if (sel) |s| {
  5641                 try self.setSelectionAndCopy(s);
  5642                 try self.queueRender();
  5643             }
  5644         },
  5645 
  5646         .inspector => |mode| return try self.rt_app.performAction(
  5647             .{ .surface = self },
  5648             .inspector,
  5649             switch (mode) {
  5650                 inline else => |tag| @field(
  5651                     apprt.action.Inspector,
  5652                     @tagName(tag),
  5653                 ),
  5654             },
  5655         ),
  5656 
  5657         .close_surface => self.close(),
  5658 
  5659         .close_window => return try self.rt_app.performAction(
  5660             .{ .surface = self },
  5661             .close_window,
  5662             {},
  5663         ),
  5664 
  5665         inline .activate_key_table,
  5666         .activate_key_table_once,
  5667         => |name, tag| {
  5668             // Look up the table in our config
  5669             const set = self.config.keybind.tables.getPtr(name) orelse {
  5670                 log.debug("key table not found: {s}", .{name});
  5671                 return false;
  5672             };
  5673 
  5674             // If this is the same table as is currently active, then
  5675             // do nothing.
  5676             if (self.keyboard.table_stack.items.len > 0) {
  5677                 const items = self.keyboard.table_stack.items;
  5678                 const active = items[items.len - 1].set;
  5679                 if (active == set) {
  5680                     log.debug("ignoring duplicate activate table: {s}", .{name});
  5681                     return false;
  5682                 }
  5683             }
  5684 
  5685             // If we're already at the max, ignore it.
  5686             if (self.keyboard.table_stack.items.len >= max_active_key_tables) {
  5687                 log.info(
  5688                     "ignoring activate table, max depth reached: {s}",
  5689                     .{name},
  5690                 );
  5691                 return false;
  5692             }
  5693 
  5694             // Add the table to the stack.
  5695             try self.keyboard.table_stack.append(self.alloc, .{
  5696                 .set = set,
  5697                 .once = tag == .activate_key_table_once,
  5698             });
  5699 
  5700             // Notify the UI.
  5701             _ = self.rt_app.performAction(
  5702                 .{ .surface = self },
  5703                 .key_table,
  5704                 .{ .activate = name },
  5705             ) catch |err| {
  5706                 log.warn(
  5707                     "failed to notify app of key table err={}",
  5708                     .{err},
  5709                 );
  5710             };
  5711 
  5712             log.debug("key table activated: {s}", .{name});
  5713         },
  5714 
  5715         .deactivate_key_table => {
  5716             switch (self.keyboard.table_stack.items.len) {
  5717                 // No key table active. This does nothing.
  5718                 0 => return false,
  5719 
  5720                 // Final key table active, clear our state.
  5721                 1 => self.keyboard.table_stack.clearAndFree(self.alloc),
  5722 
  5723                 // Restore the prior key table. We don't free any memory in
  5724                 // this case because we assume it will be freed later when
  5725                 // we finish our key table.
  5726                 else => _ = self.keyboard.table_stack.pop(),
  5727             }
  5728 
  5729             // Notify the UI.
  5730             _ = self.rt_app.performAction(
  5731                 .{ .surface = self },
  5732                 .key_table,
  5733                 .deactivate,
  5734             ) catch |err| {
  5735                 log.warn(
  5736                     "failed to notify app of key table err={}",
  5737                     .{err},
  5738                 );
  5739             };
  5740         },
  5741 
  5742         .deactivate_all_key_tables => {
  5743             return try self.deactivateAllKeyTables();
  5744         },
  5745 
  5746         .end_key_sequence => {
  5747             // End the key sequence and flush queued keys to the terminal,
  5748             // but don't encode the key that triggered this action. This
  5749             // will do that because leaf keys (keys with bindings) aren't
  5750             // in the queued encoding list.
  5751             self.endKeySequence(.flush, .retain);
  5752         },
  5753 
  5754         .crash => |location| switch (location) {
  5755             .main => @panic("crash binding action, crashing intentionally"),
  5756 
  5757             .render => {
  5758                 _ = self.renderer_thread.mailbox.push(global.io(), .{ .crash = {} }, .{ .forever = {} });
  5759                 self.queueRender() catch |err| {
  5760                     // Not a big deal if this fails.
  5761                     log.warn("failed to notify renderer of crash message err={}", .{err});
  5762                 };
  5763             },
  5764 
  5765             .io => self.queueIo(.{ .crash = {} }, .unlocked),
  5766         },
  5767 
  5768         .adjust_selection => |direction| {
  5769             self.renderer_state.mutex.lockUncancelable(global.io());
  5770             defer self.renderer_state.mutex.unlock(global.io());
  5771 
  5772             const screen: *terminal.Screen = self.io.terminal.screens.active;
  5773             const sel = if (screen.selection) |*sel| sel else {
  5774                 // If we don't have a selection we do not perform this
  5775                 // action, allowing the keybind to fall through to the
  5776                 // terminal.
  5777                 return false;
  5778             };
  5779             sel.adjust(screen, switch (direction) {
  5780                 .left => .left,
  5781                 .right => .right,
  5782                 .up => .up,
  5783                 .down => .down,
  5784                 .page_up => .page_up,
  5785                 .page_down => .page_down,
  5786                 .home => .home,
  5787                 .end => .end,
  5788                 .beginning_of_line => .beginning_of_line,
  5789                 .end_of_line => .end_of_line,
  5790             });
  5791 
  5792             // If the selection endpoint is outside of the current viewpoint,
  5793             // scroll it in to view. Note we always specifically use sel.end
  5794             // because that is what adjust modifies.
  5795             scroll: {
  5796                 const viewport_tl = screen.pages.getTopLeft(.viewport);
  5797                 const viewport_br = screen.pages.getBottomRight(.viewport).?;
  5798                 if (sel.end().isBetween(viewport_tl, viewport_br))
  5799                     break :scroll;
  5800 
  5801                 // Our end point is not within the viewport. If the end
  5802                 // point is after the br then we need to adjust the end so
  5803                 // that it is at the bottom right of the viewport.
  5804                 const target = if (sel.end().before(viewport_tl))
  5805                     sel.end()
  5806                 else
  5807                     sel.end().up(screen.pages.rows - 1) orelse sel.end();
  5808 
  5809                 screen.scroll(.{ .pin = target });
  5810             }
  5811 
  5812             // Queue a render so its shown
  5813             screen.dirty.selection = true;
  5814             try self.queueRender();
  5815         },
  5816     }
  5817 
  5818     return true;
  5819 }

  2561                 return false;
  2562             },

  2573                     log.warn("surface is not in a window, ignoring new_tab", .{});
  2574                     return false;
  2575                 };

  2577                     .command = overrides.command,
  2578                     .shell_integration = overrides.shell_integration,
  2579                     .working_directory = overrides.working_directory,
  2580                     .title = overrides.title,

n26 src/apprt/gtk/class/split_tree.zig:212–277 DIU
   212     pub fn newSplit(
   213         self: *Self,
   214         direction: Surface.Tree.Split.Direction,
   215         parent_: ?*Surface,
   216         overrides: Overrides,
   217     ) Allocator.Error!void {
   218         const alloc = Application.default().allocator();
   219 
   220         // Create our new surface.
   221         const surface: *Surface = .new(.{
   222             .command = overrides.command,
   223             .shell_integration = overrides.shell_integration,
   224             .working_directory = overrides.working_directory,
   225             .title = overrides.title,
   226         });
   227         defer surface.unref();
   228         _ = surface.refSink();
   229 
   230         // Inherit properly if we were asked to.
   231         if (parent_) |p| {
   232             if (p.core()) |core| {
   233                 surface.setParent(core, .split);
   234             }
   235         }
   236 
   237         // Bind is-split property for new surface
   238         surface.bindIsSplit(self);
   239 
   240         // Create our tree
   241         var single_tree = try Surface.Tree.init(alloc, surface);
   242         defer single_tree.deinit();
   243 
   244         // We want to move our focus to the new surface no matter what.
   245         // But we need to be careful to restore state if we fail.
   246         const old_last_focused = self.private().last_focused.get();
   247         defer if (old_last_focused) |v| v.unref(); // unref strong ref from get
   248         self.private().last_focused.set(surface);
   249         errdefer self.private().last_focused.set(old_last_focused);
   250 
   251         // If we have no tree yet, then this becomes our tree and we're done.
   252         const old_tree = self.getTree() orelse {
   253             self.setTree(&single_tree);
   254             return;
   255         };
   256 
   257         // The handle we create the split relative to. Today this is the active
   258         // surface but this might be the handle of the given parent if we want.
   259         const handle = self.getActiveSurfaceHandle() orelse .root;
   260 
   261         // Create our split!
   262         var new_tree = try old_tree.split(
   263             alloc,
   264             handle,
   265             direction,
   266             0.5, // Always split equally for new splits
   267             &single_tree,
   268         );
   269         defer new_tree.deinit();
   270         log.debug(
   271             "new split at={} direction={} old_tree={f} new_tree={f}",
   272             .{ handle, direction, old_tree, &new_tree },
   273         );
   274 
   275         // Replace our tree
   276         self.setTree(&new_tree);
   277     }

n26 src/apprt/gtk/class/split_tree.zig:713–742 DIU
   713     pub fn actionNewSplit(
   714         _: *gio.SimpleAction,
   715         args_: ?*glib.Variant,
   716         self: *Self,
   717     ) callconv(.c) void {
   718         const args = args_ orelse {
   719             log.warn("split-tree.new-split called without a parameter", .{});
   720             return;
   721         };
   722 
   723         var dir: ?[*:0]const u8 = null;
   724         args.get("&s", &dir);
   725 
   726         const direction = std.meta.stringToEnum(
   727             Surface.Tree.Split.Direction,
   728             std.mem.span(dir) orelse return,
   729         ) orelse {
   730             // Need to be defensive here since actions can be triggered externally.
   731             log.warn("invalid split direction for split-tree.new-split: {s}", .{dir.?});
   732             return;
   733         };
   734 
   735         self.newSplit(
   736             direction,
   737             self.getActiveSurface(),
   738             .none,
   739         ) catch |err| {
   740             log.warn("new split failed error={}", .{err});
   741         };
   742     }

n26 src/apprt/gtk/class/window.zig:451–498 DIU
   451     fn newTabPage(
   452         self: *Self,
   453         parent_: ?*CoreSurface,
   454         context: apprt.surface.NewSurfaceContext,
   455         overrides: Overrides,
   456     ) *adw.TabPage {
   457         const priv: *Private = self.private();
   458         const tab_view = priv.tab_view;
   459 
   460         // Create our new tab object
   461         const tab = Tab.new(
   462             priv.config,
   463             .{
   464                 .command = overrides.command,
   465                 .shell_integration = overrides.shell_integration,
   466                 .working_directory = overrides.working_directory,
   467                 .title = overrides.title,
   468             },
   469         );
   470 
   471         if (parent_) |p| {
   472             // For a new window's first tab, inherit the parent's initial size hints.
   473             if (context == .window) {
   474                 surfaceInit(p.rt_surface.gobj(), self);
   475             }
   476             tab.setParentWithContext(p, context);
   477         }
   478 
   479         // Get the position that we should insert the new tab at.
   480         const config = if (priv.config) |v| v.get() else {
   481             // If we don't have a config we just append it at the end.
   482             // This should never happen.
   483             return tab_view.append(tab.as(gtk.Widget));
   484         };
   485         const position = switch (config.@"window-new-tab-position") {
   486             .current => current: {
   487                 const selected = tab_view.getSelectedPage() orelse
   488                     break :current tab_view.getNPages();
   489                 const current = tab_view.getPagePosition(selected);
   490                 break :current current + 1;
   491             },
   492 
   493             .end => tab_view.getNPages(),
   494         };
   495 
   496         // Add the page and select it
   497         const page = tab_view.insert(tab.as(gtk.Widget), position);
   498         tab_view.setSelectedPage(page);

  1380     pub fn scope(self: Action) Scope {

  1382             // Doesn't really matter, so we'll see app.

  1490 
  1491     /// Returns a union type that only contains actions that are scoped to
  1492     /// the given scope.
  1493     pub fn Scoped(comptime s: Scope) type {
  1494         @setEvalBranchQuota(100_000);
  1495 
  1496         const all_fields = @typeInfo(Action).@"union";
  1497         const field_names = all_fields.field_names;
  1498         const field_types = all_fields.field_types;
  1499         const field_attrs = all_fields.field_attrs;
  1500 
  1501         // Find all fields that are app-scoped
  1502         var i: comptime_int = 0;
  1503         var names: [field_names.len][]const u8 = undefined;
  1504         var types: [field_names.len]type = undefined;
  1505         var attrs: [field_names.len]std.lang.Type.Union.FieldAttributes = undefined;
  1506         var raw_values: [field_names.len]comptime_int = undefined;
  1507 
  1508         inline for (field_names, field_types, field_attrs) |field_name, field_type, field_attr| {
  1509             const action = @unionInit(Action, field_name, undefined);
  1510             if (action.scope() == s) {
  1511                 names[i] = field_name;
  1512                 types[i] = field_type;
  1513                 attrs[i] = field_attr;
  1514                 raw_values[i] = i;
  1515                 i += 1;
  1516             }
  1517         }
  1518 
  1519         const TagInt = std.math.IntFittingRange(0, i);
  1520         var values: [i]TagInt = undefined;
  1521         for (raw_values[0..i], &values) |raw, *v| v.* = raw;
  1522 
  1523         // Build our union
  1524         return @Union(
  1525             .auto,
  1526             @Enum(TagInt, .exhaustive, names[0..i], &values),
  1527             names[0..i],
  1528             types[0..i],
  1529             attrs[0..i],
  1530         );
  1531     }
  1532 
  1533     /// Returns the scoped version of this action. If the action is not
  1534     /// scoped to the given scope then this returns null.
  1535     ///
  1536     /// The benefit of this function is that it allows us to use Zig's
  1537     /// exhaustive switch safety to ensure we always properly handle certain
  1538     /// scoped actions.
  1539     pub fn scoped(self: Action, comptime s: Scope) ?Scoped(s) {
  1540         switch (self) {
  1541             inline else => |v, tag| {
  1542                 // Use comptime to prune out non-app actions
  1543                 if (comptime @unionInit(
  1544                     Action,
  1545                     @tagName(tag),
  1546                     undefined,
  1547                 ).scope() != s) return null;
  1548 
  1549                 // Initialize our app action
  1550                 return @unionInit(
  1551                     Scoped(s),
  1552                     @tagName(tag),
  1553                     v,
  1554                 );
  1555             },
  1556         }
  1557     }

n26 src/terminal/PageList.zig:3347–3379 DI
  3347 pub fn scroll(self: *PageList, behavior: Scroll) void {
  3348     defer self.assertIntegrity();
  3349 
  3350     // Special case no-scrollback mode to never allow scrolling.
  3351     if (self.limits.bytes.explicit == 0) {
  3352         self.viewport = .active;
  3353         return;
  3354     }
  3355 
  3356     // Moving the viewport changes which historical pages are visible. Restart
  3357     // traversal so pages which leave the viewport are reconsidered after the
  3358     // renderer's idle delay. False positives from clamped scrolling are cheap.
  3359     defer {
  3360         self.page_compression.reset();
  3361         self.page_compression.markActivity();
  3362     }
  3363 
  3364     switch (behavior) {
  3365         .active => self.viewport = .active,
  3366         .top => self.viewport = .top,
  3367         .pin => |p| {
  3368             if (self.pinIsActive(p)) {
  3369                 self.viewport = .active;
  3370                 return;
  3371             } else if (self.pinIsTop(p)) {
  3372                 self.viewport = .top;
  3373                 return;
  3374             }
  3375 
  3376             self.viewport_pin.* = p;
  3377             self.viewport = .pin;
  3378             self.viewport_pin_row_offset = null; // invalidate cache
  3379         },

n26 src/terminal/Screen.zig:1719–1737 DIU
  1719 pub inline fn scroll(self: *Screen, behavior: Scroll) void {
  1720     defer self.assertIntegrity();
  1721 
  1722     if (comptime build_options.kitty_graphics) {
  1723         // No matter what, scrolling marks our image state as dirty since
  1724         // it could move placements. If there are no placements or no images
  1725         // this is still a very cheap operation.
  1726         self.kitty_images.markDirty();
  1727     }
  1728 
  1729     switch (behavior) {
  1730         .active => self.pages.scroll(.{ .active = {} }),
  1731         .top => self.pages.scroll(.{ .top = {} }),
  1732         .pin => |p| self.pages.scroll(.{ .pin = p }),
  1733         .row => |v| self.pages.scroll(.{ .row = v }),
  1734         .delta_row => |v| self.pages.scroll(.{ .delta_row = v }),
  1735         .delta_prompt => |v| self.pages.scroll(.{ .delta_prompt = v }),
  1736     }
  1737 }

n26 src/terminal/Screen.zig:3063–3097 DI
  3063 pub fn select(self: *Screen, sel_: ?Selection) Allocator.Error!void {
  3064     const sel = sel_ orelse {
  3065         self.clearSelection();
  3066         return;
  3067     };
  3068 
  3069     // If this selection is untracked then we track it.
  3070     const tracked_sel = if (sel.tracked()) sel else try sel.track(self);
  3071     errdefer if (!sel.tracked()) tracked_sel.deinit(self);
  3072 
  3073     // Untrack prior selection pins that aren't also owned by the replacement.
  3074     // A caller may pass our current tracked selection back to us by value, so
  3075     // releasing both old pins unconditionally would leave the replacement
  3076     // pointing at freed pool entries.
  3077     if (self.selection) |old| {
  3078         const new_bounds = tracked_sel.bounds.tracked;
  3079         switch (old.bounds) {
  3080             .untracked => old.deinit(self),
  3081             .tracked => |old_bounds| {
  3082                 if (old_bounds.start != new_bounds.start and
  3083                     old_bounds.start != new_bounds.end)
  3084                 {
  3085                     self.pages.untrackPin(old_bounds.start);
  3086                 }
  3087                 if (old_bounds.end != new_bounds.start and
  3088                     old_bounds.end != new_bounds.end)
  3089                 {
  3090                     self.pages.untrackPin(old_bounds.end);
  3091                 }
  3092             },
  3093         }
  3094     }
  3095     self.selection = tracked_sel;
  3096     self.dirty.selection = true;
  3097 }

n26 src/terminal/Screen.zig:3363–3415 DI
  3363 pub fn selectAll(self: *Screen) ?Selection {
  3364     const whitespace = &[_]u32{ 0, ' ', '\t' };
  3365 
  3366     const start: Pin = start: {
  3367         var it = self.pages.cellIterator(
  3368             .right_down,
  3369             .{ .screen = .{} },
  3370             null,
  3371         );
  3372         while (it.next()) |p| {
  3373             const cell = p.rowAndCell().cell;
  3374             if (!cell.hasText()) continue;
  3375 
  3376             // Non-empty means we found it.
  3377             const this_whitespace = std.mem.indexOfAny(
  3378                 u32,
  3379                 whitespace,
  3380                 &[_]u32{cell.content.codepoint.data},
  3381             ) != null;
  3382             if (this_whitespace) continue;
  3383 
  3384             break :start p;
  3385         }
  3386 
  3387         return null;
  3388     };
  3389 
  3390     const end: Pin = end: {
  3391         var it = self.pages.cellIterator(
  3392             .left_up,
  3393             .{ .screen = .{} },
  3394             null,
  3395         );
  3396         while (it.next()) |p| {
  3397             const cell = p.rowAndCell().cell;
  3398             if (!cell.hasText()) continue;
  3399 
  3400             // Non-empty means we found it.
  3401             const this_whitespace = std.mem.indexOfAny(
  3402                 u32,
  3403                 whitespace,
  3404                 &[_]u32{cell.content.codepoint.data},
  3405             ) != null;
  3406             if (this_whitespace) continue;
  3407 
  3408             break :end p;
  3409         }
  3410 
  3411         return null;
  3412     };
  3413 
  3414     return .init(start, end, false);
  3415 }
